
###Recopilacion de condenido del mod

####AI####
ai_strategy_unify_central_america = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/ai_strategy_icons/unification.dds"

    type = diplomatic

    will_form_power_bloc = {
        always = yes
    }

    # Infamy thresholds: más bajos que grandes potencias para forzar cautela regional
    undesirable_infamy_level = { value = 15 }
    unacceptable_infamy_level = { value = 40 }

    ideological_opinion_effect_mult = { value = 0.6 }

    # Reserve fraction for wargoal maneuvers
    wargoal_maneuvers_fraction = { value = 0.12 }

    # diplomatic play style (0 = aggressive diplomacy)
    diplomatic_play_neutrality = { value = 0 }
    diplomatic_play_boldness = { value = 50 }

    # Obligation: mayor si target comparte cultura family central_american
    obligation_value = {
        value = 0
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:target_country = {
                    OR = {
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
                    }
                }
            }
            add = 20
        }
    }

    # How reckless the AI is; increases with military tech
    recklessness = {
        value = 0.4
        if = {
            limit = { has_technology_researched = enlistment_offices } # placeholder; ajusta si tu mod usa otro tech
            add = 0.2
        }
    }

    # Base aggression and conditional modifiers
    aggression = {
        value = -0.6  # comportamiento inicial cauteloso

        # Si el target es candidato de unificación, sube agresividad
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:target_country = { is_unification_candidate = UCA }
            }
            add = 1.5
        }

        # Si target comparte cultura pero NO es candidato → leve presión diplomática
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                scope:target_country = {
                    OR = {
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
                    }
                    NOT = { is_unification_candidate = UCA }
                }
            }
            add = 0.35
        }

        # Penaliza agresividad contra países solo "antagonistic"
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_attitude = {
                    who = scope:target_country
                    attitude = antagonistic
                }
            }
            multiply = 0.4
        }

        # Ajuste por opinion ideológica (usa función engine)
        multiply = {
            value = "ai_ideological_opinion(scope:target_country)"
            multiply = -0.1
            add = 1
            max = 1.5
            min = 0.5
        }
    }

    # Edificios que prioriza para proyección regional
    building_group_weights = {
        bg_army = 1.4
        bg_infrastructure = 1.2
        bg_military_industry = 1.1
    }

    # Estados que son de alto valor para la unificación (lista basada en tus estados)
    state_value = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:target_state = {
                    OR = {
                        state_region = s:STATE_GUATEMALA
                        state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR
                        state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
                        state_region = s:STATE_HONDURAS
                        state_region = s:STATE_PANAMA
                        state_region = s:STATE_CHIAPAS
                        state_region = s:STATE_YUCATAN
                    }
                    can_reach_target_state = yes
                }
            }
            add = 90
        }
    }

    # Wargoal scoring: prioriza liderazgo en unificación y anexión de candidatos
    wargoal_scores = {
        unification_leadership = {
            value = 4
            # bonus if some "pan-national" tech/event exists
            if = {
                limit = { has_technology_researched = pan_nationalism } # placeholder
                add = {
                    value = country_fervor_primary_culture
                    divide = 3
                }
            }
            else = {
                add = {
                    value = country_fervor_primary_culture
                    divide = 8
                }
            }

            # reduce value if subjecthood exists
            if = {
                limit = { has_law = law_type:law_subjecthood }
                multiply = 0.25
            }
        }

        annex_country = {
            value = 0
            # strong incentive to annex when target is unification candidate and reachable
            if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:target_country = {
                        is_unification_candidate = UCA
                    }
                    can_reach_target_country = yes
                }
                add = 250
            }
        }
    }

    # Secret goals (local scale values)
    secret_goal_scores = {
        protect = {
            trigger = {
                scope:target_country = {
                    OR = {
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
                    }
                    NOT = { is_unification_candidate = UCA }
                    OR = {
                        is_supporting_unification_candidate = { who = root country_formation = UCA }
                        is_subject_of = root
                        is_in_customs_union_with = root
                    }
                }
            }
            score = { value = 700 }
        }

        dominate = {
            trigger = {
                scope:target_country = {
                    OR = {
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
                        country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
                    }
                    NOT = { is_unification_candidate = UCA }
                    NOR = {
                        is_supporting_unification_candidate = { who = root country_formation = UCA }
                        is_subject_of = root
                        is_in_customs_union_with = root
                    }
                }
            }
            score = { value = 600 }
        }

        conquer = {
            trigger = {
                scope:target_country = {
                    is_unification_candidate = UCA
                }
            }
            score = { value = 900 }
        }
    }

    # Quién puede elegirla
    possible = {
        is_subject = no
        is_unification_candidate = UCA
        NOT = { country_tier = empire }
    }

    # Peso base para elegir la strategy
    weight = {
        value = 6
        if = {
            limit = { has_technology_researched = pan_nationalism } # placeholder
            add = {
                value = country_fervor_primary_culture
                divide = 3
            }
        }
        else = {
            add = {
                value = country_fervor_primary_culture
                divide = 12
            }
        }

        # Increase if there is a regional unity movement (you should define movement type)
        if = {
            limit = {
                any_political_movement ?= {
                    is_political_movement_type = movement_regional_unity
                    save_temporary_scope_as = regional_unity_movement
                }
            }
            scope:regional_unity_movement ?= {
                multiply = {
                    value = 1
                    add = this.political_movement_radicalism
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
#################
# ==============================================
# ENMIENDAS CENTROAMERICANAS (CA_AMENDMENTS)
# ==============================================

# 1. LEYES DE VAGANCIA (Trabajo forzado agrícola)
# Vinculado a: Agricultura Comercial
# Efecto: Más producción agrícola, pero destruye el nivel de vida bajo y empodera terratenientes.
amendment_vagrancy_laws = {
    parent = law_commercialized_agriculture

    allowed_laws = {
        law_commercialized_agriculture
        law_tenant_farmers
    }

    modifier = {

        building_group_bg_agriculture_throughput_add = 0.15
        

        state_standard_of_living_lower_strata_add = -1.0
        

        interest_group_ig_landowners_pol_str_mult = 0.10
    }

    possible = {

        NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_regulatory_bodies }
        NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_worker_protections }
        

        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
        }
    }


    would_sponsor = {
        is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners
    }

    # La IA lo quitará si tiene leyes laborales avanzadas
    ai_will_revoke = {
        OR = {
            has_law = law_type:law_regulatory_bodies
            has_law = law_type:law_worker_protections
        }
    }

    # Requisitos para quitar la enmienda manualmente
    can_repeal = {
        legitimacy >= legitimacy_to_repeal_amendment
    }

    amendment_activism_multiplier = 0.5
}

# 2. JEFES POLÍTICOS (Caudillismo Local)
# Vinculado a: Burócratas Designados
# Efecto: Mucha autoridad y poder militar, pero corrupción (tax waste).
amendment_jefes_politicos = {
    parent = law_appointed_bureaucrats

    allowed_laws = {
        law_appointed_bureaucrats
    }

    modifier = {
        country_authority_add = 150
        

        country_tax_waste_add = 0.15
        

        interest_group_ig_armed_forces_pol_str_mult = 0.20
    }

    possible = {

        OR = {
            has_law = law_type:law_autocracy
            has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
        }
        
        # Restricción Cultural
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
        }
    }


    would_sponsor = {
        is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
    }


    ai_will_revoke = {
        OR = {
            has_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
            has_law = law_type:law_census_voting
        }
    }

    can_repeal = {
        legitimacy >= legitimacy_to_repeal_amendment
    }

    amendment_activism_multiplier = 0.75
}

bg_ca_mine = {
	category = rural
    land_usage = rural
	always_possible = yes
	can_use_slaves = yes
	cash_reserves_max = 25000
	economy_of_scale = no
	
	lens = mine
	
	urbanization = 10
	infrastructure_usage_per_level = 6
}

bg_ca_gold_field = {
	parent_group = bg_ca_mine

	auto_place_buildings = yes

	
	always_self_owning = yes
	
	pays_taxes = no

	infrastructure_usage_per_level = 0

	
	fired_pops_become_radical = no
}

bg_ca_hacienda = {
	parent_group = bg_urban_facilities

	min_productivity_to_hire = 10.0

	category = urban

	lens = trade

	always_possible = no

	can_use_slaves = yes

	auto_place_buildings = yes

	owns_other_buildings = yes

	always_self_owning = yes

	ignores_productivity_when_hiring = yes

	proportionality_limit = 1.0

	economy_of_scale = no

	default_building = ca_hacienda_generic

	cash_reserves_max = 10000

	urbanization = 0

	infrastructure_usage_per_level = 0.1

	fired_pops_become_radical = no

	should_auto_expand = {
		always = no
	}
}
bg_ca_factoria = {
	parent_group = bg_owner_buildings
}

ca_iron_mine = {
	building_group = bg_ca_mine
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/building_icons/iron_mine.dds"
	city_type = mine
	levels_per_mesh = 5
	required_construction = construction_cost_medium
	terrain_manipulator = mining
	has_max_level = yes
        possible = {
		owner = { 
			is_player = yes
			has_variable = ca_buildings_on

		}
        modifier:state_ca_iron_mine_max_level_add > 0
        }
	potential = {
		owner = { 
			is_ai = no 
			has_variable = ca_buildings_on
		}
	}
 can_build = {
        # 1. Requisito de Prospección / Estudio de Suelo
        has_variable = ca_soil_survey_complete
        
        # 2. Requisito de "Exportación" (Infraestructura lógica)
        # Esto asegura que el estado no esté aislado y pueda "exportar"
        market_access >= 0.50 # Debe tener al menos 50% de acceso al mercado
        
        # Opcional: Si es exportación marítima obligatoria
        # region_state:port > 0 
    }
possible_desc = {
        trigger = {
            NOT = { has_variable = ca_soil_survey_complete }
        }
        text = "ca_requires_soil_survey_tt" # "Requiere Estudio de Suelo"
    }	
	unlocking_technologies = {
		shaft_mining
	}

	production_method_groups = {
	    pmg_ca_mining_size_building_iron_mine
		pmg_ca_mining_equipment_building_iron_mine
		pmg_explosives_building_iron_mine
		pmg_steam_automation_building_iron_mine
		pmg_train_automation_building_iron_mine
	}
	ownership_type = self

	background = "gfx/interface/icons/building_icons/backgrounds/building_panel_bg_mining.dds"
}

    building_group = bg_ca_mine
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/building_icons/gold_mine.dds"
	city_type = mine
	levels_per_mesh = 5
	required_construction = construction_cost_medium
	terrain_manipulator = mining

        }
	potential = {
		owner = { 
			is_player = yes
			has_variable = ca_buildings_on

		}

	
	}
 can_build = {
        # 1. Requisito de Prospección / Estudio de Suelo
        has_variable = ca_soil_survey_complete
        
        # 2. Requisito de "Exportación" (Infraestructura lógica)
        # Esto asegura que el estado no esté aislado y pueda "exportar"
        market_access >= 0.50 # Debe tener al menos 50% de acceso al mercado

	unlocking_technologies = {

	ai_value = 5000 # Gold mines are very nice for minting revenue and guaranteed profits
	ai_nationalization_desire = 1.0 # AI should be keen on nationalizing these

	production_method_groups = {

		pmg_ca_mining_equipment_building_gold_mine

ca_oil_rig = {
	building_group = bg_ca_mine
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/building_icons/oil_rig.dds"
	city_type = mine
	levels_per_mesh = 5
	required_construction = construction_cost_medium
	has_max_level = yes
        possible = {
        modifier:state_ca_oil_rig_max_level_add > 0
        }
	potential = {
			owner = { 
			is_player = yes
			has_variable = ca_buildings_on
			has_technology_researched = pumpjacks
			
		}
	}
 can_build = {
        # 1. Requisito de Prospección / Estudio de Suelo
        has_variable = ca_soil_survey_complete
        
        # 2. Requisito de "Exportación" (Infraestructura lógica)
        # Esto asegura que el estado no esté aislado y pueda "exportar"
        market_access >= 0.50 # Debe tener al menos 50% de acceso al mercado
        
        # Opcional: Si es exportación marítima obligatoria
        # region_state:port > 0 
    }
	possible_desc = {
        trigger = {
            NOT = { has_variable = ca_soil_survey_complete }
        }
        text = "ca_requires_soil_survey_tt" # "Requiere Estudio de Suelo"
    }
	unlocking_technologies = {
		pumpjacks
	}

	production_method_groups = {
		pmg_base_building_oil_rig
		pmg_transportation_building_oil_rig
	}
	ownership_type = self

	background = "gfx/interface/icons/building_icons/backgrounds/building_panel_bg_oil.dds"
}

company_basic_bank = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/historical_company_icons/BasicBank.png" 
	background = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/company_backgrounds/comp_illu_manufactering_light.dds"
	
	flavored_company = yes
	
	possible_prestige_goods = {
		prestige_good_generic_currency_paper
	}
	
	building_types = {  
		building_paper_mill
		building_tooling_workshop
		building_livestock_ranch
	}
	
	extension_building_types = {	
		building_coal_mine
		building_logging_camp
		building_textile_mill
		building_furniture_manufactury
	}
	
	potential = {
		has_game_rule = banks_enabled
	}

	possible = { 
		any_owned_state = {
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_financial_district
				level >= 15
			}
		}
		has_technology_researched = investment_banks
		unique_companies_has_bank = no
	}
	
	prosperity_modifier = { 
		country_minting_mult = 0.025
		country_loan_interest_rate_mult = -0.025
        country_private_construction_allocation_mult = 0.1		
		
		state_farmers_investment_pool_contribution_add = 0.015
		state_shopkeepers_investment_pool_contribution_add = 0.015
		state_clergymen_investment_pool_contribution_add = 0.04
		state_aristocrats_investment_pool_contribution_add = 0.04
		state_capitalists_investment_pool_contribution_add = 0.065
		
		building_nationalization_investment_return_add = 0.05
		country_government_dividends_reinvestment_add = 0.05
		country_government_dividends_efficiency_add = 0.05
		
		country_max_companies_add = 1
	}
	
	ai_will_do = {
		has_technology_researched = investment_banks
		any_owned_state = {
		any_scope_building = {
			is_building_type = building_paper_mill
			level >= 3
		}
		}
	}

	ai_construction_targets = {
any_owned_state = {
            any_scope_building = {
                is_building_type = building_paper_mill
                level >= 5
            }
        }
	}
	ai_weight = {
		value = 4 # Higher base value for flavored companies
	}
}

#1. TOURON & CÍA (La Gran Oligarquía Cafetalera)
#Representa a las grandes casas exportadoras (Tournon, Rohrmoser, Lindo)
#Enfoque: Café de alta calidad y enriquecimiento de la élite.
    company_tournon_coffee = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/historical_company_icons/company_basic_agriculture_1.dds" # Usa icono genérico de café o crea uno
    background = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/company_backgrounds/comp_illu_plantation.dds"

    flavored_company = yes
    category = aristocrat_owned # Propiedad de los Terratenientes

    preferred_headquarters = { STATE_COSTA_RICA }

    building_types = {
        building_coffee_plantation
    }

    extension_building_types = {
        building_port # Controlaban sus propias exportaciones
        building_sugar_plantation
    }

    possible_prestige_goods = {
        
    prestige_good_generic_coffee # Café "Grano de Oro"
    }

    # Trigger de visibilidad (Solo para cultura costarricense o región)
    potential = {
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
        }
        has_interest_marker_in_region = region_central_america
    }

    attainable = {
        hidden_trigger = {
            any_owned_state = {
                state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
            }
        }
    }

    # Requisitos: Tecnología básica de agricultura y plantaciones activas
    possible = {
        has_technology_researched = intensive_agriculture
        any_owned_state = {
            state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
            is_incorporated = yes
            any_scope_building = {
                is_building_type = building_coffee_plantation
                level >= 5
            }
        }
    }

    # BONO: Más producción de café y poder político a los Terratenientes (Oligarquía)
    prosperity_modifier = {
        building_coffee_plantation_throughput_add = 0.20
        interest_group_ig_landowners_pol_str_mult = 0.10
        country_prestige_add = 5
    }

    ai_will_do = {
        any_owned_state = {
            state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
            has_building = building_coffee_plantation
        }
    }
}

# 2. NICARAGUA SUGAR ESTATES LTD. (Ron Flor de Caña)
# Fundada por Francisco Alfredo Pellas en 1890 (Ingenio San Antonio).
# Clave: Azúcar y Alcohol de Lujo.
company_flor_de_cana = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/historical_company_icons/company_basic_food_industry.dds"
    background = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/company_backgrounds/comp_illu_plantation.dds"

    flavored_company = yes

    preferred_headquarters = { STATE_NICARAGUA }

        building_sugar_plantation # Materia prima
        building_food_industry    # Destilería (Ron)
    }

    extension_building_types = {
        building_glassworks       # Embotellado
    }

    # Si tienes un mod que añade "Licor de Lujo", ponlo aquí.
    # Si es vanilla, el licor es un subproducto de food_industry.
    possible_prestige_goods = {
        prestige_good_liquor 
    }

    # VISIBILIDAD: Nicaragua y Centroamérica
    potential = {
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
        }
        has_interest_marker_in_region = region_central_america
    }

    attainable = {
        hidden_trigger = {
            any_owned_state = {
                state_region = s:STATE_NICARAGUA
            }
        }
    }

    # REQUISITOS (Tardíos):
    # Requiere Destilación (para el Ron) y Plantaciones grandes.
    possible = {
        has_technology_researched = distillation
        any_owned_state = {
            state_region = s:STATE_NICARAGUA
            is_incorporated = yes
            any_scope_building = {
                is_building_type = building_food_industry
                level >= 3
            }
            any_scope_building = {
                is_building_type = building_sugar_plantation
                level >= 5
            }
        }
    }

    # BONIFICADORES:
    # 1. Mucho alcohol y azúcar.
    # 2. Nivel de vida (Lujo local).

CCA = {
	states = {
		STATE_HONDURAS
		STATE_GUATEMALA		
		STATE_NICARAGUA
		STATE_SAN_SALVADOR
		STATE_PANAMA
		STATE_COSTA_RICA
	}

	required_states_fraction = 0.5
	
	ai_will_do = { always = yes }
	possible = {
	OR = {
	
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american

		}	
		
	has_technology_researched = nationalism
	save_temporary_scope_as  = central_american_confederation
	save_temporary_scope_as  = central_american_was_falling
		
	}

}
REPLACE:CRB = {
	states = { 
		STATE_CUBA
		STATE_JAMAICA
		STATE_BAHAMAS
		STATE_HAITI
		STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO
		STATE_PUERTO_RICO
		STATE_WEST_INDIES
		STATE_GUAYANA
	}

	required_states_fraction = 0.75
	
	ai_will_do = { always = yes }
}

REPLACE:ACF = {
	states = { 
		STATE_CUBA
		STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO
		STATE_PUERTO_RICO
	}

	required_states_fraction = 1
	
	ai_will_do = { always = yes }
}
GCA = {
	use_culture_states = yes
	
	is_major_formation = yes
	
	unification_play = dp_unify_central_america
	leadership_play = dp_leadership_central_america

	required_states_fraction = 0.8
	
	ai_will_do = { always = yes }

	possible = {
		OR = {
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
			AND = {
				exists = cu:guatemalan
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:el_salvadorian
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:honduran
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:nicaraguan
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:costa_rican
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:panamanian
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
			}			
			
		}	
		#has_journal_entry = je_great_republic_of_central_america
		any_country = {
			OR = {
			country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
			AND = {
				exists = cu:guatemalan
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:el_salvadorian
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:honduran
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:nicaraguan
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:costa_rican
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
			}
			AND = {
				exists = cu:panamanian
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
			}
			}	
			has_technology_researched = pan-nationalism	
		}
	}
}

ca_unrecognized = {
	is_colonizable = no
	is_unrecognized = yes
	uses_prestige = yes 
	
	has_events = yes
	
	has_military = yes
	has_economy = yes
	has_politics = yes
	can_research = yes	
	
	default_rank = unrecognized_power
	default_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
}

#diplomatic_actions
ca_defensive_pact_confederation = {
	groups= {
		general
	}
	show_in_lens = no
	can_select = no
	can_use_obligations = yes

	selectable = {
	    country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
		NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
	}
	potential = {
			scope:target_country = {
			NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
			is_revolutionary = no
		}
	}
	
	pact = {
		is_defensive_pact = yes
		
		relations_progress_per_day = 1 # At 100, +1 relations
		relations_improvement_max = 80		


		actor_can_break = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = confederation_break_defensive_pact_tt
				always = no
			}
		}

		target_can_break = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = confederation_break_defensive_pact_tt
				always = no
			}
		}

		should_invalidate = {
			OR = {
				has_war_with = scope:target_country				
				NOT = { is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_confederative_partner }
				scope:target_country = { 
					NOT = { is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_confederative_partner }
				}
				AND = {
					scope:target_country = { is_non_customs_union_subject = yes }
					scope:target_country = { is_subject_of = root }
				}
				AND = {
					is_non_customs_union_subject = yes
					is_subject_of = scope:target_country
				}
			}
		}

		manual_break_effect = {
			change_relations = { country = scope:target_country value = -30 }
			create_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 12 }
		}
		
		auto_break_effect = {
			change_relations = { country = scope:target_country value = -30 }
			create_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 12 }
		}
	}
}
ca_dependency = {
    groups = {
        general
    }
     texture = "gfx/interface/icons/lens_toolbar_icons/autonomous_province.dds"
    show_in_lens = no
    can_use_obligations = yes

    selectable = {
        OR = {
            OR = {exists = c:UCA}

            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
			
        }
     
        NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
    }

    potential = {
        scope:target_country = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
            is_revolutionary = no
        }
    }

    pact = {
        cost = 25
        has_junior_participant = yes
        recipient_pays_maintenance = no
        income_transfer = 0.2
        second_country_gets_income_transfer = no
        income_transfer_based_on_second_country = yes

        relations_progress_per_day = 1
        relations_improvement_max = 50

        target_requires_approval_to_break = yes

        subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency

        second_modifier = {
            country_convoy_contribution_to_market_owner_add = 0.5
        }

        manual_break_effect = {
            create_bidirectional_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 36 }
        }

        auto_break_effect = {
            create_bidirectional_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 36 }
        }

        actor_can_break = {
            is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
        }

        target_can_break = {
            is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
        }
    }

    ai = {
        check_acceptance_for_will_break = yes

        will_break = {
            is_subject_of = scope:target_country
            has_attitude = {
                who = scope:target_country
                attitude = rebellious
            }
        }

        propose_break_score = {
            value = 10
        }
    }
}
ca_autonomous_province = {
	groups= {
		general
	}
	show_in_lens = no
	can_select = no

    selectable = {
     country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
     
        NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
    }
	
	potential = {
		always = no
	}	

	pact = {	
		cost = 10
		is_customs_union = yes
		has_junior_participant = yes
		recipient_pays_maintenance = yes
		
		relations_progress_per_day = 0
		relations_improvement_max = 50
	
		target_requires_approval_to_break = yes	
		
		subject_type = subject_type_autonomous_province	
		
		actor_can_break = {
			is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = autonomous_province_break_tt
				always = no
			}
		}

		target_can_break = {
			is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
		}		

		break_effect = {
			create_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 12 }
		}
	}
	
	ai = {
		check_acceptance_for_will_break = yes
		check_acceptance_for_will_propose = yes
		
		will_propose = { 
			country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
			OR = {
				AND = {
					infamy < infamy_threshold:infamous
					has_attitude = {
						who = scope:target_country
						attitude = protective
					}					
				}
				AND = {
					infamy < infamy_threshold:notorious
					has_attitude = {
						who = scope:target_country
						attitude = domineering
					}				
				}			
			}
		}
	
		will_break = {
			is_subject_of = scope:target_country
			has_attitude = {
				who = scope:target_country
				attitude = rebellious
			}			
		}
		
		propose_score = {
			value = 10
		}
		
		propose_break_score = {
			value = 10
		}		
		
		accept_score = {
			value = 0
	
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_BASE"
				value = -100
			}

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ATTITUDE"
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {						
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = protective
							}
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = domineering
							}							
						}
					}
					value = 1000
				}
			}			
		}

		junior_accept_score = {
			value = 0
	
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_BASE"
				value = -100
			}	
	
			if = {
				limit = { has_truce_with = scope:actor }
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_TRUCE"
					value = -1000
				}	
			}
	
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_strategic_adjacency = scope:actor }
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_NOT_NEIGHBORS"
					value = -10
				}	
			}			

			if = {
				limit = { 
					has_strategy = ai_strategy_armed_isolationism 
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ISOLATIONIST"
					add = -25
				}
			}			
			
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_army_comparison(root)" > 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ARMY_STRENGTH"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_army_comparison(root)"
					max = 25
				}				
			}
			else = {
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ARMY_STRENGTH"
					value = -1000
				}			
			}	

			if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)" > 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_IDEOLOGICAL_OPINION"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)"
					multiply = 0.5
					max = 10
				}					
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)" < 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_IDEOLOGICAL_OPINION_NEGATIVE"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)"
					min = -30
				}					
			}			

			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = scope:actor
							type = customs_union
						}					
					}
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_NO_CUSTOMS_UNION"
					value = -10
				}	
			}					
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ATTITUDE"
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = genial
							}
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = conciliatory
							}							
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = loyal
							}	
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = cooperative
							}							
						}
					}
					value = 10
				}			
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_attitude = {
							who = scope:actor
							attitude = cautious
						}
					}
					value = -25
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = antagonistic
							}
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = domineering
							}	
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = belligerent
							}	
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = rebellious
							}
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = protective
							}							
						}
					}
					value = -1000
				}							
			}			

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_RELATIONS"	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:cordial
					}
					value = -25
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:cordial
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:amicable
					}
					value = 5
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:amicable
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:friendly
					}
					value = 10
				}	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:friendly
					}
					value = 25
				}				
			}
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_INFAMY"
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:infamous
						"scope:actor.infamy" < infamy_threshold:notorious
					}
					value = -25
				}	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:notorious
						"scope:actor.infamy" < infamy_threshold:pariah
					}
					value = -50
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:pariah
					}
					value = -100
				}					
			}					

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_RANK"
	
				if = {
					limit = { scope:actor = { country_rank > root.country_rank } }
					value = 10
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = { scope:actor = { country_rank < root.country_rank } }
					value = -10
				}				
			}	

			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_diplomatic_pact = {
						#OR = {
						#	is_diplomatic_action_type = alliance
						#	is_diplomatic_action_type = customs_union
						#	is_diplomatic_action_type = defensive_pact
						#}

						"diplomatic_pact_other_country(scope:actor)" = {
							OR = {
								country_rank >= scope:actor.country_rank
								army_size >= scope:actor.army_size
							}
						}
					}
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_POWERFUL_PROTECTORS_THEM"
					value = -25
				}	
			}		
		}	
		
		accept_break_score = {
			value = 0

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_BASE"
				value = -100
			}				
	
			if = {
				limit = { has_truce_with = scope:actor }
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_TRUCE"
					value = -1000
				}	
			}			
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ARMY_STRENGTH"
				value = "scope:actor.ai_army_comparison(root)"
				add = 0.5
				multiply = 5
				min = -50
				max = 50
			}		

			if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)" > 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_IDEOLOGICAL_OPINION"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)"
					multiply = -0.25
					min = -10
				}					
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)" < 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_IDEOLOGICAL_OPINION_NEGATIVE"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)"
					multiply = -0.25
					max = 10
				}					
			}							
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ATTITUDE"	
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_attitude = {
							who = scope:actor
							attitude = domineering
						}								
					}
					value = -50
				}							
			}			

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_RELATIONS"	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:cordial
					}
					value = -25
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:cordial
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:amicable
					}
					value = 10
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:amicable
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:friendly
					}
					value = 25
				}	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:friendly
					}
					value = 50
				}				
			}
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_INFAMY"
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:infamous
						"scope:actor.infamy" < infamy_threshold:notorious
					}
					value = -25
				}	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:notorious
						"scope:actor.infamy" < infamy_threshold:pariah
					}
					value = -50
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:pariah
					}
					value = -100
				}					
			}					

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_RANK"
	
				if = {
					limit = { scope:actor = { country_rank > root.country_rank } }
					value = 10
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = { scope:actor = { country_rank < root.country_rank } }
					value = -10
				}				
			}	

			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_diplomatic_pact = {
						OR = {
							is_diplomatic_action_type = alliance
							is_diplomatic_action_type = customs_union
							is_diplomatic_action_type = defensive_pact
						}

						"diplomatic_pact_other_country(root)" = {
							OR = {
								country_rank >= root.country_rank
								army_size >= root.army_size
							}
						}
					}
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_POWERFUL_PROTECTORS_US"
					value = 25
				}	
			}		
		}	

		use_obligation_chance = {
			value = 50
		}

		owe_obligation_chance = {
			value = 25
		}		
	}
}
ca_chartered_company = {
	groups= {
		general # for breaking only
	}
	show_in_lens = no

	selectable = {
	    country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
		NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
	}
	potential = { # Only created through diplomatic plays
		always = no
	}

	possible = {
		is_revolutionary = no
		scope:target_country = {
			is_revolutionary = no
		}
	}	
	
	pact = {
		cost = 25
	
		has_junior_participant = yes
		recipient_pays_maintenance = no
		income_transfer = 0.3
		second_country_gets_income_transfer = no
		income_transfer_based_on_second_country = yes
		
		relations_progress_per_day = 1
		relations_improvement_max = 50

		subject_type = subject_type_ca_chartered_company
		
		second_modifier = {
			country_convoy_contribution_to_market_owner_add = 0.5
		}
		
		actor_can_break = {
			is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
		}

		target_can_break = {
			is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = chartered_company_demand_independence_tt
				always = no
			}
		}
		
		manual_break_effect = {
			create_bidirectional_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 36 }
		}
		
		auto_break_effect = {
			create_bidirectional_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 36 }
		}		
	}
	
	ai = {
		evaluation_chance = {
			value = 0
		}		
	
		will_break = {
			always = no			
		}		
	}
}
ca_confederative_partner = {
	groups= {
		general
	}
	show_in_lens = no
	can_select = no

	selectable = {
	    country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
	OR ={
     country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
     }
     OR ={
     country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
     }
     OR ={
     country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
     }
     OR ={
     country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
     }
     OR ={
     country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
     }
     OR ={
     country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
     }
		NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
	}
	potential = {
		scope:target_country = {
			NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
			is_revolutionary = no
		}
	}	

	pact = {	
		cost = 25
		is_customs_union = yes
		has_junior_participant = yes
		recipient_pays_maintenance = no	
		
		relations_progress_per_day = 1
		relations_improvement_max = 50
	
		target_requires_approval_to_break = yes	
		
		subject_type = subject_type_ca_confederative_partner	
		
		actor_can_break = {
			is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = confederative_partner_break_tt
				always = no
			}
		}

		target_can_break = {
			is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
		}		

		manual_break_effect = { create_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 12 } }
		
		auto_break_effect = { create_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 12 } }
	}
	
	ai = {
		check_acceptance_for_will_break = yes
		check_acceptance_for_will_propose = yes
		
		will_propose = { 
			country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
			OR = {
				AND = {
					infamy < infamy_threshold:infamous
					has_attitude = {
						who = scope:target_country
						attitude = protective
					}					
				}
				AND = {
					infamy < infamy_threshold:notorious
					has_attitude = {
						who = scope:target_country
						attitude = domineering
					}				
				}			
			}
		}
	
		will_break = {
			is_subject_of = scope:target_country
			has_attitude = {
				who = scope:target_country
				attitude = rebellious
			}			
		}
		
		propose_score = {
			value = 10
		}
		
		propose_break_score = {
			value = 10
		}
	}
}
protectorate = {
	requires_approval = yes
	should_notify_third_parties = yes
	can_use_obligations = yes

    selectable = {
     country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian

     country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
        NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
    }
	
	possible = {
		scope:target_country = { is_at_war = no }
		has_diplomatic_relevance = scope:target_country
		is_revolutionary = no
		scope:target_country = {
			is_revolutionary = no
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = DIPLO_ACTION_MARKET_CONNECTION_REQUIRED
			OR = {	
				AND = {
					market = { has_port = yes }
					scope:target_country.market = { has_port = yes }
				}
				scope:actor.market = {
					any_scope_country = {
						is_adjacent = root
					}
				}
			}
		}		
	}

	pact = {	
		cost = 25
		#is_customs_union = yes
		has_junior_participant = yes
		recipient_pays_maintenance = no	
		
		relations_progress_per_day = 1
		relations_improvement_max = 50
	
		target_requires_approval_to_break = yes	
		
		subject_type = subject_type_protectorate	
		second_modifier = {
			country_convoy_contribution_to_market_owner_add = 0.25
		
		actor_can_break = {
			is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
		}

		target_can_break = {
			is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = no
		}		

		break_effect = {
			create_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 12 }
		}
	}
	
	accept_effect = {
		create_incident = {
			country = scope:target_country
			value = 5
		}
		create_truce = { country = scope:target_country months = 60 }
	}
	
	ai = {
		check_acceptance_for_will_break = yes
		check_acceptance_for_will_propose = yes
		
		will_propose = { 
			country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
			OR = {
				AND = {
					infamy < infamy_threshold:infamous
					has_attitude = {
						who = scope:target_country
						attitude = protective
					}					
				}
				AND = {
					infamy < infamy_threshold:notorious
					has_attitude = {
						who = scope:target_country
						attitude = domineering
					}				
				}			
			}
		}
	
		will_break = {
			is_subject_of = scope:target_country
			has_attitude = {
				who = scope:target_country
				attitude = rebellious
			}			
		}
		
		propose_score = {
			value = 10
		}
		
		propose_break_score = {
			value = 10
		}		
		
		accept_score = {
			value = 0
	
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_BASE"
				value = -100
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						is_subject = yes # subjects should not be allowed to break free and become a protectorate of another country diplomatically, and this at least ensures the AI will reject such requests
					}
				}
				add = {
					desc = "SUBJECT_IS_ALREADY_SUBJECT"
					value = -1000
				}
			}

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ATTITUDE"
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {						
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = protective
							}
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = domineering
							}							
						}
					}
					value = 1000
				}
			}			
		}

		junior_accept_score = {
			value = 0
	
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_BASE"
				value = -100
			}	
	
			if = {
				limit = { has_truce_with = scope:actor }
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_TRUCE"
					value = -1000
				}	
			}
	
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_strategic_adjacency = scope:actor }
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_NOT_NEIGHBORS"
					value = -10
				}	
			}			

			if = {
				limit = { 
					has_strategy = ai_strategy_armed_isolationism 
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ISOLATIONIST"
					add = -25
				}
			}			
			
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_army_comparison(root)" > 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ARMY_STRENGTH"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_army_comparison(root)"
					max = 25
				}				
			}
			else = {
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ARMY_STRENGTH"
					value = -1000
				}			
			}	

			if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)" > 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_IDEOLOGICAL_OPINION"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)"
					multiply = 0.5
					max = 10
				}					
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)" < 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_IDEOLOGICAL_OPINION_NEGATIVE"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)"
					min = -30
				}					
			}			

			#if = {
			#	limit = {
			#		NOT = {
			#			has_diplomatic_pact = {
			#				who = scope:actor
			#				type = customs_union
			#			}					
			#		}
			#	}
			#	add = {
			#		desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_NO_CUSTOMS_UNION"
			#		value = -10
			#	}	
			#}					
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ATTITUDE"
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = genial
							}
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = conciliatory
							}							
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = loyal
							}	
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = cooperative
							}							
						}
					}
					value = 10
				}			
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_attitude = {
							who = scope:actor
							attitude = cautious
						}
					}
					value = -25
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = antagonistic
							}
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = domineering
							}	
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = belligerent
							}	
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = rebellious
							}
							has_attitude = {
								who = scope:actor
								attitude = protective
							}							
						}
					}
					value = -1000
				}							
			}			

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_RELATIONS"	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:cordial
					}
					value = -25
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:cordial
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:amicable
					}
					value = 5
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:amicable
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:friendly
					}
					value = 10
				}	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:friendly
					}
					value = 25
				}				
			}
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_INFAMY"
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:infamous
						"scope:actor.infamy" < infamy_threshold:notorious
					}
					value = -25
				}	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:notorious
						"scope:actor.infamy" < infamy_threshold:pariah
					}
					value = -50
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:pariah
					}
					value = -100
				}					
			}					

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_RANK"
	
				if = {
					limit = { scope:actor = { country_rank > root.country_rank } }
					value = 10
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = { scope:actor = { country_rank < root.country_rank } }
					value = -10
				}				
			}	

			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						NOT = { this = scope:actor }
						OR = {
							country_rank >= scope:actor.country_rank
							army_size >= scope:actor.army_size
						}
						
						OR = {
							has_diplomatic_pact = {
								who = root
								type = alliance
							}
							#has_diplomatic_pact = {
							#	who = root
							#	type = customs_union
							#}
							has_diplomatic_pact = {
								who = root
								type = defensive_pact
							}									
						}							
					}
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_POWERFUL_PROTECTORS_THEM"
					value = -25
				}	
			}			
		}	
		
		accept_break_score = {
			value = 0

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_BASE"
				value = -100
			}				
	
			if = {
				limit = { has_truce_with = scope:actor }
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_TRUCE"
					value = -1000
				}	
			}			
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ARMY_STRENGTH"
				value = "scope:actor.ai_army_comparison(root)"
				add = 0.5
				multiply = 5
				min = -50
				max = 50
			}		

			if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)" > 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_IDEOLOGICAL_OPINION"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)"
					multiply = -0.25
					min = -10
				}					
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					"scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)" < 0
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_IDEOLOGICAL_OPINION_NEGATIVE"
					value = "scope:actor.ai_ideological_opinion(root)"
					multiply = -0.25
					max = 10
				}					
			}							
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_ATTITUDE"	
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_attitude = {
							who = scope:actor
							attitude = domineering
						}								
					}
					value = -50
				}							
			}			

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_RELATIONS"	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:cordial
					}
					value = -25
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:cordial
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:amicable
					}
					value = 10
				}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:amicable
						"relations(scope:actor)" < relations_threshold:friendly
					}
					value = 25
				}	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"relations(scope:actor)" >= relations_threshold:friendly
					}
					value = 50
				}				
			}
			
			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_INFAMY"
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:infamous
						"scope:actor.infamy" < infamy_threshold:notorious
					}
					value = -25
				}	
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:notorious
						"scope:actor.infamy" < infamy_threshold:pariah
					}
					value = -50
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						"scope:actor.infamy" >= infamy_threshold:pariah
					}
					value = -100
				}					
			}					

			add = {
				desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_RANK"
	
				if = {
					limit = { scope:actor = { country_rank > root.country_rank } }
					value = 10
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = { scope:actor = { country_rank < root.country_rank } }
					value = -10
				}				
			}	

			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						NOT = { this = root }
						OR = {
							country_rank >= root.country_rank
							army_size >= root.army_size
						}
						
						OR = {
							has_diplomatic_pact = {
								who = scope:actor
								type = alliance
							}
							#has_diplomatic_pact = {
							#	who = scope:actor
							#	type = customs_union
							#}
							has_diplomatic_pact = {
								who = scope:actor
								type = defensive_pact
							}									
						}							
					}
				}
				add = {
					desc = "DIPLOMATIC_ACCEPTANCE_POWERFUL_PROTECTORS_US"
					value = 25
				}	
			}		
		}	

		use_obligation_chance = {
			value = 50
		}

		owe_obligation_chance = {
			value = 25
		}		
	}

become_protectorate = {
	requires_approval = yes
	should_notify_third_parties = yes

	potential = {
		is_subject = no
	}
	
	possible = {
		is_at_war = no
		scope:target_country = { is_at_war = no }
		scope:target_country = {
			NOT = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = rivalry
				}					
			}					
		}
		root = {
			NOT = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = scope:target_country
					type = rivalry
				}					
			}					
		}
		has_diplomatic_relevance = scope:target_country
		is_revolutionary = no
		scope:target_country = {
			is_revolutionary = no
		}
	}
	
	reverse_pact = protectorate
	
	ai = {
		check_acceptance_for_will_propose = yes

		propose_score = {
			value = 10
		}

		propose_break_score = {
			value = 10
		}

		use_obligation_chance = {
			value = 0
		}

		owe_obligation_chance = {
			value = 0
		}
	}
}

	}

		NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }

		is_subject = no

			text = GCA_tt

		}
	}

#flag_definitions
# FLAG_DEFINITION_LIST = {		# countries search for a list with the same name as their tag, the DEFAULT list is always included, if no flag definition is applicable for a country then its tag is used a COA_KEY
# 	includes = ANOTHER_LIST		# includes another list in this list, can be repeated
# 
# 	flag_definition = {			# the flag definitions that make up this list, can be repeated
# 		coa = [list] COA_KEY	# main flag, optional list keyword denotes a coa template
# 		allow_overlord_canton = yes				# default no
# 		coa_with_overlord_canton = <[list] coa>	# flag where a canton can be placed, optional list keyword same as above, defaults to coa
# 		overlord_canton_offset = { x y }		# canton placement offset, default { 0 0 }
# 		overlord_canton_scale = { x y }			# canton placement scale, default { 0.5 0.5 }
# 		subject_canton = [list] COA_KEY	# canton applied to subjects by this country, optional list keyword same as above
# 
# 		priority = value		# valid flag definition with the highest priority applies
# 		trigger = {}			# a trigger that determines if this flag definition is valid, see below for scope
#        allow_revolutionary_indicator = no      # Default = yes. If yes, a temporary revolutionary indicator will appear while the country is revolutionary
#       revolutionary_canton = [list] COA_KEY   # Optional. Default = default_revolutionary_canton. Defines which flag should be used as canton while   this   country is revolutionary
# 	}
# }
#            | existing country | releasing a country | country formation |
# |==========|==================|=====================|===================|
# |root      | definition       | definition          | definition        |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|
# |target    | country          | N/A                 | N/A               |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|
# |initiator | N/A              | player              | player            |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|
# |actor     | country          | player              | player            |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|
# |          | country's        |                     | player's          |
# |overlord  | direct overlord  | player              | direct overlord   |
# |          | if it exists     |                     | if it exists      |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|
# common variables
@coa_width = 768
@coa_height = 512

@third = @[1/3]

CR1 = {

	flag_definition = {
		coa = CR1
		subject_canton = CR1	
		allow_overlord_canton = yes

		trigger = {

		subject_canton = ejemplo_4
		priority = 100
		trigger = {

    }
    flag_definition = {
		coa = CR1_dic_6 
		allow_overlord_canton = yes
		subject_canton = CR1_dic_6 
		priority = 50
		trigger = { 
			exists = c:CR1
			c:CR1 = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HONDURAS
					state_region = s:STATE_NICARAGUA
					state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
					state_region = s:STATE_GUATEMALA
					state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR
					state_region = s:STATE_PANAMA					
					is_incorporated = yes
					count <= 6
				}
			}
		}
	}

#government_types
gov_ca_jefe_supremo = {
	transfer_of_power = presidential_elective
	new_leader_on_reform_government = no

	male_ruler = "RULER_TITLE_PERPETUAL_DICTATOR"
	female_ruler = "RULER_TITLE_PERPETUAL_DICTATOR"


	possible = {
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_presidential_republic
		country_has_voting_franchise = yes
		has_variable = jefe_supremo_var
		AND = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
        }

	}

	on_government_type_change = {
		change_to_dictatorial = yes
	}
	on_post_government_type_change = {
		post_change_to_dictatorial = yes
	}
}

gov_ca_junta = {
	transfer_of_power = dictatorial

	male_ruler = "RULER_TITLE_GENERAL"
	female_ruler = "RULER_TITLE_GENERAL"

	possible = {
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_ca_central_american_unitary
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_ca_central_american_federal
		}
		country_has_voting_franchise = no
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_mass_conscription
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_professional_army
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			is_in_government = yes
		}

	}

	on_government_type_change = {
		change_to_dictatorial = yes
	}
	on_post_government_type_change = {
		post_change_to_dictatorial = yes
	}
}

		}
		country_has_voting_franchise = no

		change_to_dictatorial = yes
	}
	on_post_government_type_change = {
		post_change_to_dictatorial = yes
	}
}

#ideologies
# MOVIMIENTO UNITARIO (Centralismo)
# ----------------------------------------------
ideology_ca_unitarian_movement = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/ideology_icons/ideology_leader/unused/ideology_leader_caudillismo.dds"


    lawgroup_governance_principles = {
        law_ca_central_american_unitary = strongly_approve
        law_ca_central_american_federal = strongly_disapprove
        law_ca_central_american_confederated = strongly_disapprove
    }

    lawgroup_bureaucracy = { 
        law_hereditary_bureaucrats = strongly_disapprove
        law_appointed_bureaucrats = strongly_approve # Control desde la capital
        law_elected_bureaucrats = strongly_disapprove
    }
}

# ----------------------------------------------
# MOVIMIENTO FEDERALISTA 
# ----------------------------------------------
ideology_ca_federalist_movement = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/ideology_icons/ideology_leader/unused/ideology_leader_caudillismo.dds"


    lawgroup_governance_principles = {
        law_ca_central_american_unitary = strongly_disapprove
        law_ca_central_american_federal = strongly_approve
        law_ca_central_american_confederated = neutral # Aceptable como compromiso
    }

    lawgroup_bureaucracy = { 
        law_hereditary_bureaucrats = strongly_disapprove
        law_appointed_bureaucrats = disapprove
        law_elected_bureaucrats = strongly_approve # Poder local
    }
}

# ----------------------------------------------
# MOVIMIENTO AUTONOMISTA / SEPARATISTA
# ----------------------------------------------
ideology_ca_autocratic_autonomist_movement = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/ideology_icons/ideology_leader/autocratic_autonomist.dds"
    
    lawgroup_governance_principles = {
        law_ca_central_american_unitary = strongly_disapprove
        law_ca_central_american_federal = disapprove
        law_ca_central_american_confederated = strongly_approve
    }

    lawgroup_governance_principles = {
        law_monarchy = strongly_approve
        law_presidential_republic = approve
        law_parliamentary_republic = disapprove
        law_theocracy = approve
        law_council_republic = strongly_disapprove
    }

    lawgroup_distribution_of_power = {
        law_autocracy = strongly_approve

ideology_ca_liberal_monarchist = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/ideology_icons/ideology_leader/ideology_leader_royalist.dds"
    character_ideology = yes
    
    lawgroup_governance_principles = {
        law_theocracy = strongly_disapprove    
        law_monarchy = strongly_approve
        law_presidential_republic = strongly_disapprove
        law_parliamentary_republic = disapprove
        law_council_republic = strongly_disapprove
    }

    lawgroup_distribution_of_power = {
        law_autocracy = neutral 
        law_oligarchy = approve
        law_landed_voting = approve
        law_wealth_voting = approve
        law_census_voting = neutral
        law_universal_suffrage = disapprove
        law_anarchy = strongly_disapprove
    }

    lawgroup_free_speech = {
        law_outlawed_dissent = strongly_disapprove
        law_censorship = disapprove
        law_right_of_assembly = approve
        law_protected_speech = strongly_approve
    }

    lawgroup_slavery = {
        law_slavery_banned = strongly_approve
        law_legacy_slavery = disapprove
        law_debt_slavery = strongly_disapprove
        law_slave_trade = strongly_disapprove
    }
    
    #lawgroup_governance_principles = {
    #    law_ca_central_american_unitary = approve 
    #    law_ca_central_american_federal = neutral
    #    law_ca_central_american_confederated = disapprove
    #}
    country_trigger = {
        # Restringido a culturas centroamericanas
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
        }
        # Opcional: Solo si existe algún reclamo monárquico o es una monarquía
        OR = {
            has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
            has_technology_researched = egalitarianism # Requiere cierto avance político
        }
    }

    interest_group_leader_trigger = {
        # Típicamente aristócratas ilustrados o intelectuales conservadores
		ideology_ca_liberal_monarchist_valid_trigger = yes
        OR = {
            is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners
            is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
        }
    }

    non_interest_group_leader_trigger = {
        # Los agitadores no suelen ser monárquicos en repúblicas, salvo excepciones
        NOT = { has_role = agitator }
    }

    interest_group_leader_weight = {
        value = 10
        
        # Más probable si ya hay monarquía
        if = {
             owner = { has_law = law_type:law_monarchy } 
            add = 50
        }
        
        # Menos probable en repúblicas consolidadas
        if = {
            limit = { owner = { has_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic } }
            add = -50
        }
    }

    non_interest_group_leader_weight = {
        value = 5
    }	
		
}

ideology_ca_leader_autocratic_autonomist = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/ideology_icons/ideology_leader/ideology_leader_sovereignist.dds"
    character_ideology = yes

    lawgroup_governance_principles = {
        law_ca_central_american_unitary = strongly_disapprove
        law_ca_central_american_federal = strongly_disapprove
        law_ca_central_american_confederated = strongly_approve
    }

    #lawgroup_governance_principles = {
    #    law_monarchy = neutral
    #    law_presidential_republic = approve
    #    law_parliamentary_republic = disapprove
    #    law_theocracy = neutral
    #    law_council_republic = strongly_disapprove
    #}

    lawgroup_distribution_of_power = {
        law_autocracy = strongly_approve
        law_oligarchy = approve
        law_landed_voting = disapprove
        law_wealth_voting = strongly_disapprove
        law_census_voting = strongly_disapprove
        law_universal_suffrage = strongly_disapprove
        law_anarchy = strongly_disapprove
    }

    lawgroup_policing = {
        law_local_police = approve
        law_dedicated_police = disapprove
        law_militarized_police = neutral
        law_no_police = neutral
    }

    lawgroup_army_model = {
        law_national_militia = strongly_approve 
        law_professional_army = neutral
        law_mass_conscription = disapprove
        law_peasant_levies = disapprove
    }
	country_trigger = {
        # Restringido a las culturas del mod
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:los_altos # Si usas Los Altos
        }
    }

    interest_group_leader_trigger = {
        # Típico de:
        # 1. Rural Folk (Líderes campesinos regionalistas)
        # 2. Armed Forces (Jefes de milicias locales)
        # 3. Landowners (Patrones locales que no quieren gobierno central)

        OR = {
            is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk
            is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
            is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners
        }
    }

    non_interest_group_leader_trigger = {
        # Aceptamos agitadores, ya que un autonomista suele empezar agitando contra el centro
        ideology_ca_leader_autocratic_autonomist_valid_trigger = yes
    }

    interest_group_leader_weight = {
        value = 10

        # --- Lógica de Contexto Histórico ---
        
        # Más probable si el país ya es una Confederación (status quo)
        if = {
            limit = {
                owner = { has_law = law_type:law_ca_central_american_confederated }
            }
            add = 50
        }

        ## Más probable si hay caos (Radicalismo alto) -> Surgen hombres fuertes locales
        #if = {
        #    limit = {
        #        owner = { any_political_movement = { ... } }
        #    }
        #    add = 25
        #}

        # Sinergia con Milicias Nacionales
        if = {
            limit = {
                owner = { has_law = law_type:law_national_militia }
            }
            add = 25
        }

        # Rural Folk tienden a ser más autonomistas/conservadores en este contexto (tipo Carrera)
        if = {
            limit = { is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk }
            add = 20
        }
        
        # Menos probable si el país es fuertemente Unitario (ya fueron derrotados o integrados)
        if = {
            limit = {
                owner = { has_law = law_type:law_ca_central_american_unitary }
            }
            add = -50
        }
    }

    non_interest_group_leader_weight = {
        value = 10
        
        # Agitadores autonomistas aparecen si hay descontento regional
        #if = {
        #    limit = {
        #        owner = { radical_fraction > 0.15 }
        #    }
        #    add = 50
        #}
    }

}

            is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
            is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners
        }
    }

    interest_group_leader_weight = {
        value = 20

        # Muy probable para las Fuerzas Armadas
        if = {
            limit = { is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces }
            add = 100
        }

        # Más probable si hay inestabilidad o autocracia
        #if = {
        #    limit = { 
        #        owner = { 
        #            has_law = law_type:law_autocracy 
        #            radical_fraction > 0.2
        #        } 
        #    }
        #    add = 50
        #}
    }	
	
}
