#journal_entries

je_historic_central_america_falls_apart = { #
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	
	group = je_group_historical_content	

	should_be_pinned_by_default = yes

    #frca_guatemala_peace_var
    #frca_nicaragua_peace_var
    #frca_honduras_peace_var
    #frca_costarica_peace_var
	#frca_elsalvador_peace_var
	
	scripted_button = je_restore_order_gua_button
	scripted_button = je_restore_order_hon_button
	scripted_button = je_restore_order_nic_button
	scripted_button = je_restore_order_cos_button
	scripted_button = je_restore_order_els_button
	scripted_button = je_nicaragua_intervention_central_america_button
	scripted_button = je_costa_rica_intervention_central_america_button
	scripted_button = je_honduras_intervention_central_america_button
	scripted_button = je_guatemala_intervention_central_america_button
	scripted_button = je_el_salvador_intervention_central_america_button
	scripted_button = federal_congress_decree_button
	immediate = {
	set_variable = { name = frca_support value = 0 scope:journal_entry = root }
	#c:UCA = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.14 days = 3 } } 
	}
	current_value = {
        value = root.var:frca_support
    }
	scripted_progress_bar = frca_support_bar
	progress_desc = je_frca_support_progress_desc
	progressbar = yes
	goal_add_value = {
        add = 100
    }
	weight = 100	
	on_weekly_pulse = {
		events = {
			#Civil War Result
			#Civil War Result
			#Central American Dissolution -> Confederation via Conservatives in government
		}
		calculate_frca_support_event_progress = yes
	}
	
	on_monthly_pulse = {
		events = {
            ca_central_america.14 #The Livingston Penal Code
            ca_central_america.1 #A local newspaper proposed the formation of a state that would include the regions of Quetzaltenango, Totonicapán, Sololá and Suchitepéquez.
            ca_central_america.3 #Establishment of Los Altos 5 de junio de 1838
			ca_central_america.5 #The Fate of Soconusco
			ca_central_america.15 #Cholera Pandemic Hits Central America
            ca_central_america.20 #Separatism in Guatemala
			ca_central_america.19 #Separatism in Nicaragua  
			ca_central_america.46 #Nicaragua Declares Independence
			ca_central_america.18 #Separatism in Costa Rica  
			ca_central_america.45 #Costa Rica declares independence
			ca_central_america.42 #Union Collapses Into Civil War / #Accept Fate & Become El Salvador			
			ca_central_america.21 #Separatism in Honduras  
			ca_central_america.48 #Honduras Declares Independence      
			ca_central_america.69 #Civil War in Guatemala  
			ca_central_america.23 #Separatism in Los Altos
			ca_central_america.85 #Los Altos Declares Independence
			ca_central_america.47 #Guatemala Declares Independence
			ca_central_america.63 #Civil War in Nicaragua
		}
		random_events = {
			99 = 0
			25 = ca_central_america.17 #Cholera Spreads In Central America
		}
	 
	}
	complete = {
             custom_tooltip = {
                 text = frca_support_complete
                 scope:journal_entry = {
                     root.var:frca_support >= 100
                 }
             }
		OR = {
			custom_tooltip = { 
				text = uca_won_war_tt
				OR = {
					has_variable = frca_won_war #Misleading var name, it's used also if you just dissolve the country or do something else that is accounted for
					AND = {
						has_variable = second_frca_civil_war_var
						is_at_war = no
						is_active_in_diplomatic_play = no
						NOT = { exists = c:GUA }
						NOT = { exists = c:HON }
						NOT = { exists = c:NIC }
						NOT = { exists = c:COS }
						NOT = { exists = c:ELS }
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = guatemala_support_tt
					has_variable = gua_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:GUA }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = el_salvador_support_tt
					has_variable = els_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:ELS }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = honduras_support_tt
					has_variable = hon_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:HON }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = nicaragua_support_tt
					has_variable = nic_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:NIC }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = costa_rica_support_tt
					has_variable = cos_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:COS }
				}
                any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_GUATEMALA
					state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR
					state_region = s:STATE_HONDURAS
					state_region = s:STATE_NICARAGUA
					state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
				}
				#count < 5				
			}
		}
      }		

					
	}
	
	fail = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = central_america_fail_tt
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_GUATEMALA
					state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR
					state_region = s:STATE_HONDURAS
					state_region = s:STATE_NICARAGUA
					state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
				}
				count < 3
			}
		}
		OR = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = recognized_guatemalan_independence_tt
					exists = c:GUA
					c:GUA = {has_variable = recognized_guatemalan_independence}
					 
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = recognized_el_salvador_independence_tt
					exists = c:ELS					
					c:ELS ={has_variable = recognized_el_salvador_independence}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = recognized_honduran_independence_tt
					exists = c:HON						
					c:HON ={has_variable = recognized_honduran_independence}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = recognized_nicaraguan_independence_tt
					exists = c:NIC					
					c:NIC ={has_variable = recognized_nicaraguan_independence}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = recognized_costa_rican_independence_tt
					exists = c:COS					
					c:COS ={has_variable = recognized_costa_rican_independence}
				}		
		#any_scope_state = {	
		#	state_region = s:STATE_GUATEMALA
		#	#turmoil < 0.20
		#	has_variable = recognized_guatemalan_independence
		#}
		#any_scope_state = {	
		#	state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR
		#	#turmoil < 0.20
		#	has_variable = recognized_el_salvador_independence
		#}
		#
		#any_scope_state = {	
		#	state_region = s:STATE_HONDURAS
		#	#turmoil < 0.20	
		#    has_variable = recognized_honduran_independence			
		#}
		#
		#any_scope_state = {	
		#	state_region = s:STATE_NICARAGUA
		#	#turmoil < 0.20
        #    has_variable = recognized_nicaraguan_independence			
		#}
		#	
		#
		#any_scope_state = {	
		#	state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
		#	#turmoil < 0.20
		#	has_variable = recognized_costa_rican_independence
		#}		
		
		}
		OR = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = frca_lost_war_tt
				has_variable = frca_lost_war
			}
		}
		
	}
	
	on_fail = {
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.13 popup = yes }
		custom_tooltip = central_america_capital_tt
	    hidden_effect = { 
			ig:ig_landowners = {
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
				add_ideology = ideology_caudillismo
			}
		
			ig:ig_armed_forces = {
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
			}

			ig:ig_devout = {
				add_ideology = ideology_paternalistic
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
			}
			remove_variable = is_frca_var
		}
	}

	on_complete = {
		
		remove_variable = uca_cholera_pandemic
		hidden_effect = { 
			ig:ig_landowners = {
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
				add_ideology = ideology_caudillismo
			}
		
			ig:ig_armed_forces = {
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
			}

			ig:ig_devout = {
				add_ideology = ideology_paternalistic
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
			}
			
			#trigger_event = { id = "the_grand_union" } 
		}
	}
	

	
	transferable = no

	invalid = {
		NOT = { exists = c:UCA }
	}

}

je_ca_federalists_vs_separatists_situation = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_flags/event_uca_flag.dds"
	
	group = je_group_historical_content	

	should_be_pinned_by_default = yes
	current_value = {
		value = root.var:federalists_vs_separatists_var
	}	
   weight = 100

	calculate_federalists_vs_separatists_progress = yes
	immediate = {
	set_variable = { name = federalists_vs_separatists_next value = 0 scope:journal_entry = root }
	#c:UCA = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.14 days = 3 } } 
	}
	
	complete = { #Federalists Win , The Play and AI Chosing
		ig:ig_intelligentsia = {
			is_in_government = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_federalists_vs_separatists_goal_tt
			scope:journal_entry = {
				is_goal_complete = yes
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = federalists_vs_separatists_complete
			root.var:federalists_vs_separatists_var >= 100
		}
		
	}
	on_weekly_pulse = {
		effect = {
            
        }
	}
	on_monthly_pulse = {
		events = {
			
		}
	}

	on_yearly_pulse = {
		effect = {

		}
	}	
	on_complete = {
			ig:ig_landowners = {
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
				add_ideology = ideology_caudillismo
			}
		
			ig:ig_armed_forces = {
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
			}

			ig:ig_devout = {
				add_ideology = ideology_paternalistic
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
			}
		if = {  #Nicaragua Beats to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:NIC ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.47 days = 3 }}

		}
		if = {  #Costa rica Beats to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:COS ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.50 days = 3 }}

		}

		if = {  #Honduras Beats to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:HON ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.53 days = 3 }}

		}

		if = {  #Guatemala Beats to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:GUA ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.59 days = 3 }}

		}

		if = {  #Los Altos Beats to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:ALT ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.62 days = 3 }}

		}

		if = {  #El Salvador Beats to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:ELS ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.56 days = 3 }}

		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			remove_modifier = age_of_caudillos_modifier
		}	
	}
	
	fail = { #Separatists Win, The Play and AI Chosing
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			is_in_government = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = federalists_vs_separatists_complete_fail
			root.var:federalists_vs_separatists_var <= 0
		}		
	}
	
	on_fail = {
       	ig:ig_landowners = {
				remove_ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary
				add_ideology = ideology_caudillismo
			}
		
			ig:ig_devout = {
				add_ideology = ideology_paternalistic
			}
		if = {  #Nicaragua falls to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:NIC ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia2
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var2
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.48 days = 3 }}

		}
		if = {  #Costa rica falls to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:COS ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia2
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var2
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.51 days = 3 }}

		}
		if = {  #Honduras falls to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:HON ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia2
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var2
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.54 days = 3 }}

		}
		if = {  #Guatemala falls to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:GUA ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia2
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var2
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.60 days = 3 }}

		}
		if = {  #Los Altos falls to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:ALT ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia2
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var2
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.63 days = 3 }}

		}
		if = {  #El Salvador  falls to secessionists
			limit = {
				c:ELS ?= this
				NOR = { 
					has_journal_entry = je_francocracia2
					has_variable = death_of_francia_var2
				}
			}
			c:UCA = {trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.57 days = 3 }}

		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = age_of_caudillos_modifier
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		
	}

	transferable = no

	invalid = {
		NOT = { exists = c:UCA }
	}
	
}
je_ca_agro_export_boom = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/goods_icons/coffee.dds"
    group = je_group_economic_development
    should_be_pinned_by_default = yes

    # ACTIVACIÓN
    possible = {
        # Cualquier país de cultura centroamericana
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
        }
        # Que no sea una gran potencia aún
        rank_value < 50
    }

    # CONDICIONES DE COMPLETADO (Objetivos Claros)
    complete = {
        # 1. Tener una base productiva fuerte
        # (Suma de niveles de café + fruta + azúcar > 30)
        calc_building_level_count:building_coffee_plantation >= 15
        calc_building_level_count:building_fruit_plantation >= 10
        
        # 2. Infraestructura para exportar
        calc_building_level_count:building_port >= 5
        
        # 3. Política Comercial: NO Aislacionismo
        NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_isolationism }
    }

    # RECOMPENSA
    on_complete = {
        # Evento de celebración
        trigger_event = { id = ca_economy.1 }
        
        # Modificador permanente de eficiencia comercial
        add_modifier = {
            name = modifier_ca_agro_export_miracle
            months = 180 # 15 años de bonanza
        }
    }
}

# =========================================================
# 2. MILITAR: EL FIN DE LOS CAUDILLOS
# Objetivo: Crear un ejército nacional organizado.
# Referencia: Similar a las reformas militares europeas.
# =========================================================
je_ca_military_reform = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/law_icons/professional_army.dds"
    group = je_group_military
    
    possible = {
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
        }
        # Tienen leyes arcaicas
        has_law = law_type:law_peasant_levies
    }

    complete = {
        # 1. Eliminar las levas campesinas
        OR = {
            has_law = law_type:law_professional_army
            has_law = law_type:law_national_militia
            has_law = law_type:law_mass_conscription
        }
        
        # 2. Tener una academia militar
        # (Asumiendo que tienes un edificio de universidad renombrado o usas la tech)
        has_technology_researched = general_staff
        
        # 3. Tener un ejército decente (para el tamaño de la región)
        # > 10 batallones regulares
        calc_regular_battalion_count >= 10
        
        # 4. Los militares deben ser leales (no golpistas)
        ig:ig_armed_forces = {
            approval >= 5
        }
    }

    on_complete = {
        trigger_event = { id = ca_military.1 }
        add_modifier = {
            name = modifier_ca_professional_army_bonus
        }
    }
}

# =========================================================
# 3. CULTURAL: ILUSTRACIÓN CENTROAMERICANA
# Objetivo: Alfabetizar a la población y reducir la influencia clerical.
# =========================================================
je_ca_enlightenment = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/jomini/literacy_icon.dds"
    group = je_group_internal_affairs
    
    possible = {
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
        }
        literacy_rate < 0.40 # Solo visible si la alfabetización es baja
    }

    complete = {
        # 1. Meta de Alfabetización (Alta para la región en 1836)
        literacy_rate >= 0.40
        
        # 2. Institución Educativa activa
        has_institution = institution_schools
        institution_investment_level:institution_schools >= 3
        
        # 3. Tener al menos una Universidad
        calc_building_level_count:building_university >= 1
        
        # 4. Intelectuales influyentes
        ig:ig_intelligentsia = {
            is_powerful = yes
            is_in_government = yes
        }
    }

    on_complete = {
        trigger_event = { id = ca_culture.1 }
        add_modifier = {
            name = modifier_ca_enlightenment_era
        }
    }
}

je_national_geological_survey = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
    
   
    progressbar = ca_prospecting_bar
    
   
    possible = {
        is_ca_culture_country = yes
        has_technology_researched = prospecting 
    }
    
    
    active = {
        is_ca_culture_country = yes
    }
    
    complete = {

        has_variable = ca_survey_progress
        var:ca_survey_progress >= 100
    }
    

    scripted_button = "fund_survey_expedition"
    scripted_button = "hire_foreign_geologists"

    on_complete = {
  
        set_variable = ca_can_build_mines_globally # Ejemplo de variable maestra
        

        trigger_event = { id = ca_prospecting.100 }
    }
    
    weight = 100
}

#laws
law_ca_comunal_milicia = {
	group = lawgroup_army_model
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/law_icons/peasant_levies.dds"

	progressiveness = -50

	is_visible = {
	        exists = c:UCA
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
        }
	}

	can_enact = {
	}


	modifier = {
		country_can_only_conscript_peasants_bool = yes
		unit_morale_loss_mult = 0.1
		unit_experience_gain_mult = -0.25
		state_conscription_rate_add = 0.04
		country_aristocrats_pol_str_mult = 0.25
        interest_group_ig_landowners_pol_str_mult = 0.25		
		country_military_goods_cost_mult = -0.1
		state_building_barrack_max_level_add = 25
		state_building_conscription_center_max_level_add = 25
	
	}

	can_impose = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_in_same_power_bloc = scope:target_country
				AND = {
					modifier:country_can_impose_same_lawgroup_army_model_in_power_bloc_bool = yes
					has_law = scope:law
				}
			}
			can_impose_law_default = yes
		}
	}

	on_enact = {
		custom_tooltip = QUALIFICATIONS_OFFICERS_PEASANT_LEVIES_DESC
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_peasant_levies } }
            custom_tooltip = aristocrats_af_ig_shift_effect_tt
        }		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_peasant_levies
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = aristocrats_af_ig_shift_effect_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_professional_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = military_pops_af_ig_shift_effect_negative_tt
			custom_tooltip = non_military_pops_af_ig_shift_effect_negative_tt
		}
	}

	# AI will never return to peasant levies
	ai_will_do = {
		always = no
	}

	ai_enact_weight_modifier = { #Petitions
		value = 0

		if = {
			limit = { ai_has_enact_weight_modifier_journal_entries = yes }
			add = 750
		}
	}
}
law_ca_caudillismo = {
	group = lawgroup_army_model
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/law_icons/peasant_levies.dds"

	progressiveness = -50

	is_visible = {
	        exists = c:UCA
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
        }
	}

	modifier = {
		country_can_only_conscript_peasants_bool = yes
		unit_morale_loss_mult = 0.1
		unit_experience_gain_mult = -0.25
		state_conscription_rate_add = 0.04
		country_aristocrats_pol_str_mult = 0.25
		country_military_goods_cost_mult = -0.1
		state_building_barrack_max_level_add = 25
		state_building_conscription_center_max_level_add = 25
	}

	can_impose = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_in_same_power_bloc = scope:target_country
				AND = {
					modifier:country_can_impose_same_lawgroup_army_model_in_power_bloc_bool = yes
					has_law = scope:law
				}
			}
			can_impose_law_default = yes
		}
	}

	on_enact = {

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_peasant_levies
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = aristocrats_af_ig_shift_effect_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_professional_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = military_pops_af_ig_shift_effect_negative_tt
			custom_tooltip = non_military_pops_af_ig_shift_effect_negative_tt
		}
	}

	# AI will never return to peasant levies
	ai_will_do = {
		always = no
	}

	ai_enact_weight_modifier = { #Petitions
		value = 0

		if = {
			limit = { ai_has_enact_weight_modifier_journal_entries = yes }
			add = 750
		}
	}
}
law_regionalist_militia = {
	group = lawgroup_army_model
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/law_icons/national_militia.dds"

	is_visible = {
	        exists = c:UCA
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
        }
	}

	progressiveness = 0


	modifier = {
		unit_morale_loss_mult = -0.05
		state_conscription_rate_add = 0.05
		country_leverage_resistance_mult = 0.5
		country_officers_pol_str_mult = -0.25
		state_building_barrack_max_level_add = 5
		state_building_conscription_center_max_level_add = 100
		
	}

	can_enact = {
		OR = {
			is_power_bloc_leader = no
			power_bloc ?= {
				NOT = { has_identity = identity:identity_military_treaty_organization }
			}
		}
	}

	on_enact = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_peasant_levies
			}
			custom_tooltip = aristocrats_af_ig_shift_effect_negative_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_professional_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = military_pops_af_ig_shift_effect_negative_tt
			custom_tooltip = non_military_pops_af_ig_shift_effect_negative_tt
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		exists = ruler
		ruler = {
			has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pacifist
		}
	}

	ai_enact_weight_modifier = { #Petitions
		value = 0

		if = {
			limit = { ai_has_enact_weight_modifier_journal_entries = yes }
			add = 750
		}
	}
}

law_mixed_economy = { #Better interventionnism, allow to unlock nearly all ownership PMs
	group = lawgroup_economic_system
	
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/law_icons/mixed_economy.dds"
	
	progressiveness = 100
	
	disallowing_laws = {
		law_serfdom
		law_anarchy
	}
	
	on_activate = {
	}
	
	unlocking_technologies = {
		macroeconomics
	}
	
	modifier = {
		country_private_construction_allocation_mult = 0.5
		state_shopkeepers_investment_pool_efficiency_mult = 0.1	
		state_capitalists_investment_pool_efficiency_mult = 0.1
		country_minting_mult = 0.25
		state_tax_capacity_mult = 0.1
		country_authority_mult = 0.25 #more taxes
		state_bureaucracy_population_base_cost_factor_mult = 0.1
		country_max_companies_add = 1
		building_nationalization_cost_mult = -0.25
		building_nationalization_radicals_mult = -0.5
		building_nationalization_investment_return_add = 0.75
		country_government_dividends_reinvestment_add = 0.75
		country_government_dividends_efficiency_add = 0.5
		country_free_charters_add = 2
		country_construction_add = -5 #modern law so no need for free construction
	}

	ai_enact_weight_modifier = { #Petitions
		value = 0

		if = {
			limit = { ai_has_enact_weight_modifier_journal_entries = yes }
			add = 750
		}
	}

	ai_impose_chance = {
		value = 0

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_mixed_economy
			}

			add = base_impose_law_weight
			multiply = 10.0
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target_country = {
					c:BIC ?= this
					OR = {
						has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_plantations
						has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_military
						has_journal_entry = je_british_dictate_universities
					}
				}
			}
			multiply = 0
		}
	}	
}
law_central_american_agrarianism = { #Agroexportadores
	group = lawgroup_economic_system
    parent = law_agrarianism
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/law_icons/agrarianism.dds"

	progressiveness = 0

	is_visible = {
	        exists = c:UCA
        OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
        }
	}

	disallowing_laws = {
		law_anarchy
	}

	unlocking_technologies = {
		romanticism
	}

	on_activate = {
	}

	modifier = {
		state_aristocrats_investment_pool_efficiency_mult = 0.30
		state_clergymen_investment_pool_efficiency_mult = 0.15
		state_farmers_investment_pool_efficiency_mult = 0.25
		state_capitalists_investment_pool_efficiency_mult = -0.30
		country_private_construction_allocation_mult = 0.85
		state_bureaucracy_population_base_cost_factor_mult = -0.13
		building_nationalization_investment_return_add = 0.35
		country_government_dividends_reinvestment_add = 0.35
		country_government_dividends_efficiency_add = 0.15
		country_free_charters_add = 1
		country_construction_add = -2.5 #less free construction
        country_disable_nationalization_bool = yes
		country_disable_nationalization_without_compensation_bool = yes
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		OR = {
			has_law = law_type:law_traditionalism
			literacy_rate < 0.4
		}
	}

	ai_enact_weight_modifier = { #Petitions
		value = 0

		if = {
			limit = { ai_has_enact_weight_modifier_journal_entries = yes }
			add = 750
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_sick_man_bureaucracy
				has_law = law_type:law_traditionalism
			}
			add = 10000 # Top priority for Tanzimat AI is to get out of Traditionalism
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_great_reforms_serfdom
			}
			add = 1000 # Less preferred than either laissez-faire or interventionism
		}

		# AI should really try to get out of Traditionalism under the right circumstances
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_traditionalism
				NOT = { has_strategy = ai_strategy_reactionary_agenda }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_strategy = ai_strategy_conservative_agenda }
					any_political_movement = {
						law_enactment_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_agrarianism
							value > neutral
						}
						political_movement_support >= 0.10
					}
				}
			}
			add = 1000
		}
	}
}

# LEY FEDERAL (Status Quo / Histórico)
# Equilibrio: Bonos a la lealtad local, penalización a la autoridad central.
# ==============================================
law_ca_central_american_federal = {
    group = lawgroup_governance_principles
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/law_icons/placeholder.dds" # Asegúrate de tener este icono o usa uno genérico
    
    progressiveness = 50


    is_visible = {
        OR = {
            OR = {exists = c:UCA}

            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
	    law_ca_valid_trigger = yes
        }
    }


    can_enact = {
        NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_theocracy }
		law_ca_valid_trigger = yes
    }

    requires_law_or = {
        law_presidential_republic
    }

    modifier = {

        country_authority_add = -25 
        
        country_legitimacy_base_add = 50
        state_standard_of_living_add = 0.5
        political_movement_radicalism_add = -0.10
        country_institutions_cost_mult = -0.10
    }

    ai_enact_weight_modifier = {
        value = 50
        if = {
            limit = { has_ideology = ideology_ca_federalist }
            add = 100
        }
    }
}

#on_actions
on_tech_researched = {
    effect = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_ca_culture_country = yes
                has_technology_researched = prospecting
                NOT = { has_variable = ca_survey_progress }
            }
            set_variable = {
                name = ca_survey_progress
                value = 0
            }
        }
    }
}
on_country_released_as_independent = {
    effect = {
        
        if = {
            limit = {
               
                has_dlc_feature = ip4_content
                                
                is_historical_spanish_american_colony = yes
                               
                NOT = { has_modifier = modifier_not_recognized_by_spain }
                
                c:SPA ?= { always = yes }

                
                NOT = { 
                    any_overlord_or_above = { c:SPA ?= this }
                }
            }


            add_modifier = {
                name = modifier_not_recognized_by_spain
            }


            add_journal_entry = {
                type = je_spanish_american_independence
            }
        }
    }
}
on_secession_country_created = {
    effect = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_dlc_feature = ip4_content
                is_historical_spanish_american_colony = yes
                NOT = { has_modifier = modifier_not_recognized_by_spain }
                c:SPA ?= { always = yes }
                NOT = { any_overlord_or_above = { c:SPA ?= this } }
            }
            add_modifier = { name = modifier_not_recognized_by_spain }
            add_journal_entry = { type = je_spanish_american_independence }
        }
    }
}
on_game_start_country = {
    effect = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_ca_culture_country = yes
                has_technology_researched = prospecting
                NOT = { has_variable = ca_survey_progress }
            }
            set_variable = {
                name = ca_survey_progress
                value = 0
            }
        }
    }
}
on_game_started = {
    effect = {
        # Llama al script de configuración de Centroamérica
        ca_initial_game_start_setup = yes
    }
}

#political_lobbies
lobby_pro_federalist = {
    category = foreign_pro_country
    texture = "gfx/interface/icons/political_lobby_icons/lobby_pro.dds"
    
    can_create = {
        only_centralamericans_cultures = yes 
        

        scope:target_country = { c:UCA ?= this }
        

        is_subject_of = scope:target_country
 
        is_subject_type = ca_dependency 
        
        is_revolutionary = no
    }
    
    # FACTORES DE FELICIDAD
    appeasement_factors_pro = {
        appeasement_factors_pro_federalist
    }
    
    appeasement_factors_anti = {
        appeasement_factors_anti_federalist
    }
    
    
    join_weight = {
       
        if = {
            limit = { 
                exists = scope:political_lobby
                scope:political_lobby = { any_lobby_member = { this = root } }
            }    
            add = { value = 50 desc = "member_of_lobby" }
        }    

        
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_ideology = ideology_ca_federalist_movement # O tu ideología de líder federalista
            }
            add = { value = 100 desc = "ideologically_federalist" }
        }

        
        scope:target_country = {
            add = {
                every_active_law = {
                    if = {
                        limit = { ROOT = { law_stance = { law = PREV.type value >= approve } } }
                        add = 5
                    }
                }
                desc = "shared_values_with_uca"
            }
        }
        
        
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners # A veces los hacendados exportadores quieren federalismo
            }
            add = {
                desc = "economic_dependence_on_federation"
                owner ?= {
                    value = "economic_dependence(scope:target_country)"
                    multiply = 10
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    
    on_created = {
        save_temporary_scope_as = lobby_country
        scope:political_lobby ?= {
            change_appeasement = {
                amount = pro_overlord_lobby_starting_appeasement 
                factor = appeasement_diplomatic_status_quo
            }
            initialize_lobby_custom_name = yes        
        }
    }
}
lobby_anti_federalist = {
    category = foreign_anti_country
    texture = "gfx/interface/icons/political_lobby_icons/lobby_anti.dds"
    

    can_create = {
        only_centralamericans_cultures = yes
        

        scope:target_country = { c:UCA ?= this }
        is_subject_type = ca_dependency
        is_revolutionary = no
    }
    

    appeasement_factors_pro = {
        appeasement_factors_anti_federalist 
    
    appeasement_factors_anti = {
        appeasement_factors_pro_federalist 
    }
    

    join_weight = {
        if = {
            limit = { 
                exists = scope:political_lobby
                scope:political_lobby = { any_lobby_member = { this = root } }
            }    
            add = { value = 50 desc = "member_of_lobby" }
        }  

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    has_ideology = ideology_ca_unitarian_movement
                    has_ideology = ideology_ca_autocratic_autonomist
                }
            }
            add = { value = 100 desc = "ideologically_separatist" }
        }


        if = {
            limit = {
                is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
                owner = { has_law = law_state_religion } # Nosotros somos religiosos
                scope:target_country = { has_law = law_total_separation } # La UCA es atea/secular
            }
            add = { value = 75 desc = "religious_incompatibility" }
        }


        if = {
            limit = { is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie }
            add = { value = 25 desc = "local_nationalism" }
        }
    }
    
    on_created = {
        save_temporary_scope_as = lobby_country
        scope:political_lobby ?= {
            change_appeasement = {
                amount = anti_overlord_lobby_starting_appeasement
                factor = appeasement_diplomatic_status_quo
            }
            initialize_lobby_custom_name = yes            
        }    
    }
}

lobby_pro_federalist = {
	category = foreign_pro_country

	texture = "gfx/interface/icons/political_lobby_icons/lobby_pro.dds"
	
	can_create = {
       only_centralamericans_cultures = yes
		NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
		is_revolutionary = no
		scope:target_country = { is_revolutionary = no }
	}
	
	requirement_to_maintain = {
		trigger = { is_subject_of = scope:target_country }
		
		swap_type_on_failed = lobby_pro_country
		
		on_failed = {
			scope:political_lobby = { initialize_lobby_custom_name = yes }
		}
	}
	
	requirement_to_maintain = {
		trigger = { 
			OR = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:political_lobby }
				scope:political_lobby ?= { 
					any_lobby_member = { is_marginal = no } 
				} 				
			}
		}
		
		on_failed = { }
	}

	on_created = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = lobby_country
		scope:political_lobby ?= {
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = pro_overlord_lobby_starting_appeasement
			   factor = appeasement_diplomatic_status_quo
			}
			
			initialize_lobby_custom_name = yes		
		}
	}
	
	appeasement_factors_pro = {
		appeasement_relations_increased	
		appeasement_autonomy_decreased
		appeasement_became_subject
		appeasement_knowledge_sharing_started
		appeasement_support_regime_started
	}	
	
	appeasement_factors_anti = {
		appeasement_relations_decreased	
		appeasement_autonomy_increased
		appeasement_gained_independence	
		appeasement_independence_supported
		appeasement_knowledge_sharing_ended
		appeasement_support_regime_ended
	}
	
	available_for_interest_group = {
		NOT = {
			any_political_lobby = {
				target = scope:target_country
			}
		}
	}
	
	join_weight = {
		### Stickyness factor if already in lobby
		if = {
			limit = { 
				exists = scope:political_lobby
				scope:political_lobby = { any_lobby_member = { this = root } }
			}	
			add = {
				value = 50
				desc = "member_of_lobby"
			}
		}	
	
		if = {
			limit = { 
				scope:target_country = {
					any_interest_group = {
						is_same_interest_group_type = ROOT
						is_in_government = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add = {
				value = 10
				desc = "preferred_igs_in_government"
			}
		}	

		scope:target_country = {
			add = {
				every_active_law = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							ROOT = {
								law_stance = {
									law = PREV.type
									value >= approve
								}
							}
						}
						add = 5
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							ROOT = {
								law_stance = {
									law = PREV.type
									value <= disapprove
								}
							}
						}
						add = -5
					}
				}
				
				max = 100
				min = -100
				
				if = {
					limit = { is_lobby_formation_reason_ideology = yes }
					multiply = 1.5
				}
				
				desc = "enacted_laws"
			}
		}

		### Culture
		add = {
			if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_patriotic
						leader ?= {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ethno_nationalist
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
							}
						}
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:target_country = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								is_primary_culture_of = ROOT.owner
							}
						}
					}
					add = 50 # Settler Colonies
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:target_country = {
							NOT = {
								any_primary_culture = {
									OR = {
										shares_heritage_trait_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT.owner
										shares_non_heritage_trait_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT.owner
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					add = -15
				}
			}

			desc = "primary_culture_similarity"
		}

		### Religion
		add = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
						leader ?= {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_theocrat
								has_trait = pious
							}
						}
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:target_country = {
							religion = ROOT.owner.religion
						}
					}
					add = 15
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:target_country = {
							discriminates_religion = ROOT.owner.religion
						}
					}
					add = -15
				}
			}

			desc = "religion_opinion"
		}

		# Communists
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target_country = {
					has_law = law_type:law_council_republic
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_council_republic
						value <= disapprove
					}
				}

				add = {
					value = -200
					desc = "anti_communism"
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_council_republic
						value < approve
					}
				}

				add = -100
				desc = "baseline_opposition_to_communism"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { # Equals 'count'
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_council_republic
							value > strongly_approve
						}
					}
				}

				add = {
					value = 200
					desc = "communist_solidarity"
				}
			}
		}
		
		min = 0
		
		# Pacts
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target_country = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = scope:country
						type = fund_lobbies
					}
				}	
			}

			multiply = {
				value = 1.25
				desc = "funding_lobbies"		
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target_country = {
					any_rivaling_country = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = scope:country
							type = fund_lobbies
						}					
					}
				}	
			}

			multiply = {
				value = 0.75
				desc = "rival_funding_lobbies"		
			}
		}			

		### Overlord Relations
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
					is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "economic_dependence_for_upper_classes"
				owner ?= {
					value = "economic_dependence(scope:target_country)"
					multiply = 10
					max = 50
					min = 0
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
			}
			add = {
				desc = "economic_dependence_for_middle_classes"
				owner ?= {
					value = "economic_dependence(scope:target_country)"
					multiply = 5
					max = 25
					min = 0
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = yes
			}

			scope:target_country = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						relations:root < 0
					}

					multiply = {
						value = 0.5
						desc = "relations_negative"
					}
				}
			}

			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						liberty_desire <= 25
					}
				}

				multiply = {
					value = 1.05
					desc = "low_liberty_desire"
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						add = -5
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							ROOT = {
								law_stance = {
									law = PREV.type
									value <= disapprove
								}
							}

					multiply = 1.5
				}				

				desc = "enacted_laws"
			}
		}

					limit = {
						scope:target_country = {
							any_primary_culture = {
								is_primary_culture_of = ROOT.owner
							}
						}
					}
					add = -25
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:target_country = {
							NOT = {
								any_primary_culture = {
									OR = {
										shares_heritage_trait_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT.owner
										shares_non_heritage_trait_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT.owner
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					add = 15
				}
			}

			desc = "primary_culture_similarity"
		}

		### Religion
		add = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
						leader ?= {
							OR = {
								has_ideology = ideology:ideology_theocrat
								has_trait = pious
							}
						}
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:target_country = {
							religion = ROOT.owner.religion
						}
					}
					add = -15
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:target_country = {
							discriminates_religion = ROOT.owner.religion
						}
					}
					add = 25
				}

				# Catholics
				if = {
					limit = {
						owner = {
							country_has_state_religion = rel:catholic
						}
						scope:target_country = {
							country_has_state_religion = rel:catholic
							c:PAP ?= THIS
						}
					}
					add = 25
				}
			}

			desc = "religion_opinion"
		}

		### Ideologies
		# Communists
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target_country = {
					has_law = law_type:law_council_republic
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_council_republic
						value <= disapprove
					}
				}

				add = {
					value = 200
					desc = "anti_communism"
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_council_republic
						value < approve
					}
				}

				add = {
					value = 100
					desc = "baseline_opposition_to_communism"
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { # Equals 'count'
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_council_republic
							value > strongly_approve
						}
					}
				}

				add = {
					value = -50
					desc = "communist_solidarity"
				}
			}
		}

		# Ideological Republicans
		if = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican
					leader ?= {
						OR = {
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_leader
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
							has_ideology = ideology:ideology_positivist
						}
					}
				}
				scope:target_country = {
					has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
				}
			}
			add = {
				value = 25
				desc = "republican_anti_monarchism"
			}
		}

		min = 0
		
		# Pacts
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target_country = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = scope:country
						type = fund_lobbies
					}
				}	
			}

			multiply = {
				value = 0.5
				desc = "funding_lobbies"		
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target_country = {
					any_rivaling_country = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = scope:country
							type = fund_lobbies
						}					
					}
				}	
			}

			multiply = {
				value = 1.25
				desc = "rival_funding_lobbies"		
			}
		}		

		### Overlord Relations
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk
					is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "economic_dependence_for_lower_classes"
				owner ?= {
					value = "economic_dependence(scope:target_country)"
					multiply = 10
					max = 50
					min = 0
				}
			}
		}

		add = {
			desc = "from_liberty_desire"
			owner ?= {
				value = liberty_desire
				multiply = 0.75
				max = 75
				min = 0
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = yes
				scope:target_country = {
					relations:root < 0
				}
			}
			multiply = {
				value = 1.5
				desc = "relations_negative"
			}
		}
	}
}

#political_movements
# ==============================================
# MOVIMIENTO LIBERAL (Reformistas / Modernizadores)
# ==============================================
movement_ca_liberal = {
    category = movement_category_ideological
    ideology = ideology_ca_liberal_movement

    character_ideologies = {
        ideology_market_liberal
        ideology_liberal_leader
        ideology_republican_leader
        ideology_radical
        ideology_humanitarian
        ideology_ca_liberal_monarchist
        ideology_ca_secularist
        ideology_ca_criollo_liberal
        ideology_ca_criollo_elite
    }

    creation_trigger = {
        NOT = {
            any_political_movement = {
                is_political_movement_type = movement_ca_liberal
            }
        }
        only_centralamericans_cultures = yes
    }

    creation_weight = {
        value = 100

        if = {
            limit = {
                has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
                country_has_voting_franchise = no
            }
            multiply = 0.1
        }
    }

    on_created = {
        add_movement_enthusiasm_modifier = yes
    }

 
    character_support_trigger = {
        OR = {
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_liberal_monarchist 
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_secularist 
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_criollo_liberal 
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_criollo_elite 
            

            scope:interest_group ?= {
                OR = {
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_liberal
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_liberal_modern
                }
            }
        }
    }

    character_support_weight = {
        value = 200

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_leader
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_liberal_leader
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_market_liberal
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
                }
            }
            multiply = 5
        }

        else_if = {
            limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_modernizer_leader }
            multiply = 2
        }
    }


    pop_support_trigger = {
        NOT = { is_pop_type = slaves }
    }

    pop_support_factors = {
        movement_support_high_urbanization
        movement_support_high_literacy
        movement_support_capitalists
        movement_support_academics
        movement_support_bureaucrats
        movement_support_clerks
    }


    revolution = {
        possible = { political_movement_support >= 0.05 }
        weight = { value = 100 }
        state_weight = {
            value = 1
            if = {
                limit = { owner = { ig:intelligentsia = { is_powerful = yes } } }
                add = 50
            }
        }
        target_fraction_of_states = { value = 0.35 }
    }
    

}

# ==============================================
# MOVIMIENTO REACCIONARIO (Tradicionalistas)
# Objetivo: Revolución (si fuertes) o Secesión (si ignorados)
# ==============================================
movement_ca_reactionary = {
    category = movement_category_ideological
    ideology = ideology_central_american_reactionary_movement 

    character_ideologies = {
        ideology_traditionalist
        ideology_protectionist
        ideology_authoritarian
        ideology_luddite
        ideology_central_american_reactionary
        ideology_central_american_reactionary_2
        ideology_ca_criollo_paternalistic
        ideology_ca_criollo_stratocratic
    }

    creation_trigger = {
        NOT = {
            any_political_movement = { is_political_movement_type = movement_ca_reactionary }
        }
                OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
        }
    }

    creation_weight = { value = 100 }
    on_created = { add_movement_enthusiasm_modifier = yes }


    character_support_trigger = {
        OR = {
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_protectionist
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_authoritarian
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_central_american_reactionary
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_central_american_reactionary_2
            
            scope:interest_group ?= {
                OR = {
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_reactionary
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_patriarchal
                }
            }
        }
    }

    character_support_weight = {
        value = 200
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_central_american_reactionary
                }
            }
            multiply = 5
        }
    }

    pop_support_factors = {
        movement_support_aristocrats
        movement_support_clergymen
        movement_support_farmers
        movement_support_peasants # Base campesina fuerte (tipo Carrera)
    }

    
    # Opción A: Guerra Civil para tomar el poder
    revolution = {
        possible = { political_movement_support >= 0.10 }
        weight = { value = 100 }
    }

    # Opción B: Secesión (Independencia local)
    secession = {
        possible = {
            political_movement_support >= 0.10
            scope:clout <= 1 # Si no tienen voz en el gobierno, se van.
        }
        weight = { value = 1000 }
        state_weight = {
            value = state_urbanization_rate
            multiply = -1 # Más probable en zonas rurales
        }
    }
}

movement_ca_unitarian = {

    }

    creation_trigger = {

            any_political_movement = { is_political_movement_type = movement_ca_unitarian }
        }
                OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
        }
    }

    creation_weight = { value = 100 }
    on_created = { add_movement_enthusiasm_modifier = yes }


    character_support_trigger = {
        OR = {
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_unitarian
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_central_america_caudillo
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_authoritarian
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_criollo_paternalistic
        }
    }

    character_support_weight = {
        value = 200
        if = {
            limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_unitarian }
            multiply = 5
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_central_america_caudillo }
            multiply = 3
        }
    }
    

    pop_support_factors = {
        movement_support_soldiers
        movement_support_officers
        movement_support_bureaucrats
        movement_support_aristocrats
    }

    revolution = {
        possible = { political_movement_support >= 0.05 }
        weight = { value = 150 } # Son agresivos
    }
}

            any_political_movement = { is_political_movement_type = movement_ca_federalist }
        }
                OR = {
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:central_american
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            country_has_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
        }
    }

    creation_weight = { value = 100 }
    on_created = { add_movement_enthusiasm_modifier = yes }


    character_support_trigger = {
        OR = {
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_federalist
            has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_constitutionalist
            
            scope:interest_group ?= {
                OR = {
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_liberal
                    has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican
                    is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
                }
            }
        }
    }

    character_support_weight = {
        value = 200
        if = {
            limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_federalist }
            multiply = 5
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_leader }
            multiply = 2
        }
    }
    
    pop_support_factors = {
        movement_support_academics
        movement_support_clerks
        movement_support_shopkeepers # Clase media urbana
    }


    revolution = {
        possible = { political_movement_support >= 0.05 }
        weight = { value = 100 }
    }
}

federal_congress_decree_button = {
	name = "federal_congress_decree_button"
	desc = "federal_congress_decree_button_desc"
	
	visible = { 
	NOT = {
	has_valiable  = voting_ca_self_determination}
}	
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = ""
			NOT = {
				has_variable = ca_self_determination
			}
		}

	}
	effect = {
     ca_self_determination_decret = yes
	}
}

fragile_unity_decree_button = {
	name = "fragile_unity_decree_button2"
	desc = "fragile_unity_decree_button_desc2"

	visible = { always = yes }

	possible = {
		ig:ig_armed_forces = {
			is_in_government = yes
			is_powerful = yes
		}
		NOT = { has_modifier = caudillo_rule_by_decree_modifier }
	}

	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = gain_caudillo_points
			change_variable = {
				name = fragile_unity_var
				add = 100
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = caudillo_rule_by_decree_modifier
			months = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

#Historic events##
je_nicaragua_intervention_central_america_button = {
	name = nicaragua_intervention_central_america_button
	desc = nicaragua_intervention_central_america_button_desc

	visible = { 
		NOT = { has_variable = je_central_america_setup_nic_var }
		exists = c:NIC
		c:NIC ?= {
			is_subject_of = ROOT
		}
	}

	possible = {
		is_at_war = no
		is_active_in_diplomatic_play = no
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = nicaragua_subject_frca_tt
			c:NIC ?= {
				is_subject_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = invade_nicaragua_frca_stab_tt
			this = c:UCA
			exists = c:NIC
			has_variable = nic_support_var
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = invade_nicaragua_frca_nic_support_tt
			this = c:UCA
			exists = c:NIC
		}
	}

	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = invade_nicaragua_frca_tt
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				name = nicaraguan_intervention
				type = dp_annex_war
				escalation = 20
				target_state = s:STATE_NICARAGUA.region_state:NIC
			
			}
	}

	ai_chance = {
		base = 0
	}
}

je_restore_order_gua_button = {
	name = restore_order_gua_button
	desc = restore_order_gua_button_desc

	visible = {
		NOT = { has_variable = je_central_america_setup_gua_var }
		NOT = { exists = c:GUA }
		has_variable = uca_morazan_invades_gua_var
	}

	possible = {
		NOT = { exists = c:GUA }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = controls_gua_tt
			this = c:UCA
			NOT = { has_variable = gua_support_var }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = uca_restore_order_cooldown_tt
			NOT = { has_variable = uca_recentled_restored_order_var }
		}
	}

	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = restore_order_gua_tt
			set_variable = gua_support_var
			set_variable = {
				name = uca_recentled_restored_order_var
				days = 60
			}
		}
	}

	ai_chance = {
		base = 100
	}
}

	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = restore_order_cos_tt
			set_variable = cos_support_var
			set_variable = {
				name = uca_recentled_restored_order_var
				days = 60
			}
		}
	}

	ai_chance = {
		base = 100
	}
}

fund_survey_expedition = {
    name = "FUND_SURVEY_EXPEDITION"
    desc = "FUND_SURVEY_EXPEDITION_DESC"
    
    possible = {
        has_variable = ca_survey_progress
        var:ca_survey_progress < 100
        gold_reserves >= 2000
    }
    
    effect = {
        add_treasury = -2000
        
        # Sube la barra +10 puntos
        change_variable = {
            name = ca_survey_progress
            add = 10
        }
    }
    
    ai_chance = { base = 10 }
}

hire_foreign_geologists = {
    name = "HIRE_FOREIGN_GEOLOGISTS"
    desc = "HIRE_FOREIGN_GEOLOGISTS_DESC"
    
    possible = {
        has_variable = ca_survey_progress
        var:ca_survey_progress < 100
        gold_reserves >= 5000
    }
    
    effect = {
        add_treasury = -5000
        add_radicals = { value = 1000 } # Los locales se enojan un poco
        
        # Sube la barra +25 puntos
        change_variable = {
            name = ca_survey_progress
            add = 25
        }
    }
    
    ai_chance = { base = 5 }
}

#scripted_effects
calculate_federalists_vs_separatists_progress = {
	set_variable = { name = federalists_vs_separatists_next value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			ig:ig_intelligentsia = {
				is_powerful = yes
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = 1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ig:ig_intelligentsia = {
				is_in_government = yes
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = 1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ig:ig_intelligentsia  = {
				is_marginal = yes
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = -5
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ig:ig_intelligentsia = {
				is_in_government = no
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = -2
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_law = law_type:law_federal
			
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = 10
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_law = law_type:law_devolved
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = -10
		}
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ig:ig_landowners = {
				is_powerful = yes
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = 2
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ig:ig_landowners = {
				is_in_government = yes
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = 1
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
			ig:ig_landowners  = {
				is_marginal = yes
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = 2
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ig:ig_landowners = {
				is_in_government = no
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = federalists_vs_separatists_next
			add = 1
		}
	}

	


}
calculate_frca_support_event_progress = {
	
		if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = gua_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:GUA }
					NOT = { has_variable = je_gua_support_var }
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = 1 }
			}
		if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = gua_support_var }
						exists = c:GUA
					}
					has_variable = je_gua_support_var
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = -1 }
			}
		if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = els_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:ELS }
					NOT = { has_variable = je_els_support_var }
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = 1 }
			}
		if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = els_support_var }
						exists = c:ELS
					}
					has_variable = je_els_support_var
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = -1 }
			}
		if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = hon_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:HON }
					NOT = { has_variable = je_hon_support_var }
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = 1 }
			}
		if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = hon_support_var }
						exists = c:HON
					}
					has_variable = je_hon_support_var
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = -1 }
			}
		if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = nic_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:NIC }
					NOT = { has_variable = je_nic_support_var }
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = 1 }
			}
		if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = nic_support_var }
						exists = c:NIC
					}
					has_variable = je_nic_support_var
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = -1 }
			}
		if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = cos_support_var
					NOT = { exists = c:COS }
					NOT = { has_variable = je_cos_support_var }
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = 1 }
			}
		if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = cos_support_var }
						exists = c:COS
					}
					has_variable = je_cos_support_var
				}
				change_variable = { name = frca_support add = -1 }
			}


	    if = {
	     	limit = {
	     		exists = c:ELS
	     		has_variable = frca_elsalvador_peace_var
	     	}
	     	change_variable = { name = frca_support add = -5 }
	     }
	    if = {
	     	limit = {
	     		exists = c:HON
	     		has_variable = frca_honduras_peace_var
	     	}
	     	change_variable = { name = frca_support add = -5 }
	     }
	    if = {
	     	limit = {
	     		exists = c:NIC
	     		has_variable = frca_nicaragua_peace_var
	     	}
			change_variable = { name = frca_support add = -5 }
	     }
	    if = {
	     	limit = {
	     		exists = c:COS
	     		has_variable = frca_costarica_peace_var
	     	}
	     	change_variable = { name = frca_support add = -5 }
	     }



}

ca_setup_ideological_strongholds_effect = {
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # EL SALVADOR: La Capital Federal y Liberal
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR = { 
        every_scope_state = { # Transición de State Region a State
            add_modifier = { name = federal_stronghold }
            add_modifier = { name = liberal_stronghold }
        }
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # GUATEMALA: La Vieja Élite y el Caudillismo
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    s:STATE_GUATEMALA = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = unitary_stronghold }
            add_modifier = { name = conservative_stronghold }
            add_modifier = { name = reactionary_stronghold }
        }
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # NICARAGUA: División y Separatismo
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    s:STATE_NICARAGUA = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = separatism_stronghold }
            add_modifier = { name = liberal_stronghold_small_nation }
            add_modifier = { name = conservative_stronghold_small_nation }
        }
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # COSTA RICA: Aislacionismo y Orden
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    s:STATE_COSTA_RICA = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = separatism_stronghold }
            add_modifier = { name = unitary_stronghold_small_nation }
        }
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # HONDURAS: Federalismo en Decadencia
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    s:STATE_HONDURAS = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = waning_federal_stronghold }
            add_modifier = { name = conservative_stronghold_small_nation }
        }
    }
}

ca_setup_political_movements_effect = {
    s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_liberal_stronghold }
        }
    }

    s:STATE_GUATEMALA = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_conservative_capital }
        }
    }

    s:STATE_COSTA_RICA = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_isolated_autonomists }
        }
    }

    s:STATE_HONDURAS = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_federalist_heartland }
        }
    }

    s:STATE_PANAMA = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_isthmian_liberalism }
        }
    }

    s:STATE_NICARAGUA = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_leones_vs_granadinos }
        }
    }

    s:STATE_CHIAPAS = {
        every_scope_state = {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_frontier_tensions }
        }
    }
}

ca_setup_state_trait_mining_effect = {
    s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR = {
        add_state_trait = state_trait_mining_els
    }

    s:STATE_GUATEMALA = {
        add_state_trait = state_trait_mining_gua
    }

    s:STATE_COSTA_RICA = {
        add_state_trait = state_trait_mining_cos
    }

    s:STATE_HONDURAS = {
        add_state_trait = state_trait_mining_hon
    }

    s:STATE_NICARAGUA = {
        add_state_trait = state_trait_mining_nic
    }
}
ca_initial_game_start_setup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_setup_done }
		}
		ca_setup_political_movements_effect = yes
		ca_setup_ideological_strongholds_effect = yes
		ca_setup_state_trait_mining_effect = yes
		
		set_global_variable = ca_setup_done
	}
}

frca_support_bar = {
	name = "my_frca_support_bar_name"
	desc = "my_frca_support_bar_desc"
	second_desc = "PROGRESS_BAR_TEXT3"
	
	start_value = 60
	min_value = 0
	max_value = 100
	
	double_sided_bad = yes

	monthly_progress = {
		# ==========================
		# GUATEMALA
		# ==========================
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = gua_support_var
				NOT = { exists = c:GUA }
				NOT = { has_variable = je_gua_support_var }
			}
			add = {
				desc = "gua_support_DESC1"

			}

				}

			}

				desc = "gua_support_DESC2"

		}

		# ==========================

ca_golden_grain_bar = {
	name = "ca_golden_grain_bar_name"
	desc = "ca_golden_grain_bar_desc"
	
	default_green = yes
	start_value = 0
	min_value = 0
	max_value = 100

	monthly_progress = {
		# 1. Producción Base
		if = {
			limit = {
				# CORRECCIÓN: Para medir niveles, verificamos que en algún estado haya café nivel > 10
				any_owned_state = {
					state_building_level = {
						building = building_coffee_plantation
						value >= 10
					}
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "golden_grain_production_base"
				value = 1
			}
		}

		# 2. Infraestructura
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_technology_researched = railways
				any_owned_state = {
					has_building = building_coffee_plantation
					has_building = building_railway
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "golden_grain_infrastructure"
				value = 1
			}
		}

		# 3. Exportaciones
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_law = law_type:law_free_trade
					has_law = law_type:law_mercantilism
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "golden_grain_exports"
				value = 1
			}
		}

		# 4. Apoyo de Oligarquía
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_landowners = {
					is_powerful = yes
					ig_approval > 5 # CORRECCIÓN: ig_approval en vez de approval
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "golden_grain_oligarchy_support"
				value = 0.5
			}
		}

		# 5. Penalización: Falta de Mano de Obra
		if = {
			limit = {
				# CORRECCIÓN: Evaluamos la ocupación del edificio específico, no del grupo.
				any_owned_state = {
					has_building = building_coffee_plantation
					building_employment_fraction = {
						building = building_coffee_plantation
						value < 0.75
					}
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "golden_grain_labor_shortage"
				value = -2
			}
		}
	}
}

federalists_vs_separatists_progress_core_progress_bar = {
	name = "federalists_vs_separatists_progress_core_progress_bar_name"
	desc = "federalists_vs_separatists_progress_core_progress_bar_federalists_desc"
	second_desc = "federalists_vs_separatists_progress_core_progress_bar_separatists_desc"

	double_sided_gold = yes

	start_value = 100
	min_value = 0
	max_value = 200

	monthly_progress = {
		# ===============================================
		# FACTORES FEDERALISTAS (+)
		# ===============================================
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_intelligentsia = { is_in_government = yes }
			}
			add = {
				desc = "federalists_intelligentsia_in_gov"
				value = 1
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_ca_central_american_federal
			}
			add = {
				desc = "federalists_law_active"
				value = 0.5
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				legitimacy >= 90
			}
			add = {
				desc = "federalists_high_legitimacy"
				value = 0.5
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					leader = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ca_federalist # CORRECCIÓN: Prefijo ideology: añadido
					}
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "federalist_ig_leader_bonus"
				value = 3
			}
		}

		# ===============================================
		# FACTORES SEPARATISTAS (-)
		# ===============================================
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_landowners = { 
					is_powerful = yes
					ig_approval < -5 # CORRECCIÓN
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "separatists_angry_landowners"
				value = -2
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_devout = {
					is_powerful = yes
					ig_approval < -5 # CORRECCIÓN
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "separatists_angry_church"
				value = -1
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				legitimacy < 25
			}
			add = {
				desc = "separatists_low_legitimacy"
				value = -1
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				
				any_owned_state = {
					state_region = { is_homeland = cu:central_american }
					state_turmoil > 0.25
				}
			}
			add = {
				desc = "separatists_local_turmoil"
				value = -1
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = voting_ca_self_determination
			}
			add = {
				desc = "separatists_may_30_decree_active"
				value = -10 
			}
		}        
	}
}

# Uso: country_event
ca_reduce_support_separatism_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = frca_support }
		change_variable = {
			name = frca_support
			subtract = 10 # Balanceable desde un solo archivo
		}
	}
}

		limit = { has_variable = frca_support }
		change_variable = {
			name = frca_support
			subtract = 25
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = frca_support }
		change_variable = {
			name = frca_support
			add = 15
		}
	}
}

#scripted_triggers
#            | existing country | releasing a country | country formation |
# |==========|==================|=====================|===================|
# |root      | definition       | definition          | definition        |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|
# |target    | country          | N/A                 | N/A               |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|
# |initiator | N/A              | player              | player            |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|
# |actor     | country          | player              | player            |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|
# |          | country's        |                     | player's          |
# |overlord  | direct overlord  | player              | direct overlord   |
# |          | if it exists     |                     | if it exists      |
# |----------|------------------|---------------------|-------------------|

### scope is COUNTRY ###

# Government


coa_def_central_american_ensign_trigger = {
	exists = c:UCA
	c:UCA ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = custom_overlord }
	coa_def_ensign_trigger = yes
}

coa_def_frca_ensign_trigger = {
	exists = c:UCA
	c:UCA = { has_variable = is_frca_var }
	c:UCA ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = custom_overlord }
	coa_def_ensign_trigger = yes
}

coa_def_soconosco_uca_trigger = {
	exists = c:CIP
	c:CIP = { 
		is_subject_of = c:UCA
		NOT = { owns_entire_state_region = STATE_CHIAPAS } 
	}
}

coa_def_soconosco_trigger = {
	exists = c:CIP
	c:CIP = { 
		NOT = { owns_entire_state_region = STATE_CHIAPAS } 
	}
	OR = {
		c:CIP = { is_subject_of = c:ALT }
		c:CIP = { is_subject_of = c:GUA }
	}
}

#subject_types
subject_type_ca_dependency = {
	allow_change_country_flag = yes
	use_overlord_map_color = yes
	use_overlord_ruler = no
	annex_on_country_formation = yes
	can_start_own_diplomatic_plays = no
	breaks_if_subject_not_protected = yes
	join_overlord_wars = yes
	overlord_must_be_higher_rank = yes
	overlord_must_be_same_country_type = no
	use_for_release_country = yes
	gives_prestige_to_overlord = yes
    autonomy_level = 1
    category = same_as_puppet
	
	diplomatic_action = ca_dependency

	higher_autonomy_subject_type_alternatives = {
        subject_type_ca_protectorate
        subject_type_ca_chartered_company
	}
		
	valid_overlord_country_types = {
		recognized
		colonial
	}
	
	valid_subject_country_types = {
		recognized
		colonial
		unrecognized
		native_american_comun
		decentralized
	}		
	
	valid_overlord_ranks = {
		great_power
		major_power
		minor_power
	}
	
	valid_subject_ranks = {
		major_power
		minor_power	
		insignificant_power
		unrecognized_major_power
		unrecognized_regional_power	
		unrecognized_power
	}
}
subject_type_ca_protectorate = {
	allow_change_country_flag = no
	use_overlord_map_color = no
	use_overlord_ruler = no
	annex_on_country_formation = no
	can_start_own_diplomatic_plays = yes
	breaks_if_subject_not_protected = yes
	join_overlord_wars = no
	can_have_subjects = yes
	overlord_must_be_higher_rank = yes
	overlord_must_be_same_country_type = no
	use_for_release_country = no
	gives_prestige_to_overlord = yes
	can_offer_to_become_in_sway_offers = yes
		
	diplomatic_action = ca_protectorate
	
    autonomy_level = 2
    category = same_as_puppet

	higher_autonomy_subject_type_alternatives = {
        subject_type_ca_confederative_partner
        subject_type_ca_autonomous_province
	}

	lower_autonomy_subject_type_alternatives = {
		subject_type_ca_dependency
	}
	
		
	valid_overlord_country_types = {
		recognized
		colonial
	}
	
	valid_subject_country_types = {
		recognized
		colonial
		unrecognized
	}	

	valid_overlord_ranks = {
		great_power
		major_power
		minor_power
	}
	
	valid_subject_ranks = {
		minor_power
		insignificant_power
		unrecognized_regional_power	
		unrecognized_power
	}
	
	# Multiplies both subject_value and become_subject_value in AI strategies
	# Higher value means AI is more interested in having this type of subject but less interested in *being* this type of subject
	# Cannot return a value lower than MIN_SUBJECT_TYPE_VALUE
	ai_value = {
		value = 0
	}
}
